rules/API.md

Raw file

API — public JSON readback / filter / escaping / nulls

R-API-001

- ID: R-API-001

- Statement: GET /api/tasks returns a JSON array of task rows read straight from the DB, outside the authenticated UI path (the filter exempts /api/). It is reachable with no session at all.

- Source citations: ApiTaskServlet.java:14-17,23-49; AuthFilter.java:28

- Actors and preconditions: Any client, authenticated or not.

- Trigger: GET /api/tasks.

- Effects or refusal: 200 application/json UTF-8 array; no mutation.

- Observed versus intended: OBSERVED: unauthenticated readback. INTENDED per source comment ("public read-back ... outside the UI path"); flagged in defect ledger (D-007 context) for the verifier to confirm the exposure is acceptable.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-API-001

R-API-002

- ID: R-API-002

- Statement: Optional status param filters exactly like the UI list: empty/absent -> all tasks (findAll, updated_at DESC); non-empty -> search(status,null,null); unknown status values return an empty array, HTTP 200. No other filter or sort params are honored (assignee/sort are ignored).

- Source citations: ApiTaskServlet.java:24-27; TaskDao.java:22-24,40-56

- Actors and preconditions: Any client.

- Trigger: GET /api/tasks?status=DONE, ?status=, ?status=BOGUS, ?assignee=3&sort=title.

- Effects or refusal: DONE-only array / full array / [] / full array (extra params ignored).

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-API-002

R-API-003

- ID: R-API-003

- Statement: Each object exposes exactly id, title, status, priority, assigneeId, projectId, dueDate, updatedAt; description and the display-join names are NOT exposed; updatedAt is emitted with seconds precision.

- Source citations: ApiTaskServlet.java:36-45; TaskDao.java:234-236

- Actors and preconditions: Any client.

- Trigger: GET /api/tasks.

- Effects or refusal: Fixed field set per row; no extra fields.

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-API-003

R-API-004

- ID: R-API-004

- Statement: NULL handling: assigneeId NULL -> JSON literal null; dueDate NULL -> JSON literal null; strings that are NULL would be escaped to "" but no exposed string column is nullable in practice (title/status/priority/updatedAt are NOT NULL).

- Source citations: ApiTaskServlet.java:41,43; esc() 51-54; schema.sql:30-39

- Actors and preconditions: Any client; a task with unassigned/no-due-date (seed task 4).

- Trigger: GET /api/tasks.

- Effects or refusal: "assigneeId":null,"dueDate":null for task 4.

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-API-004

R-API-005

- ID: R-API-005

- Statement: String escaping is minimal hand-rolled esc(): only backslash and double-quote are escaped. Control characters (notably newline) and HTML-significant characters pass through raw, so a title containing a newline produces invalid JSON; </> are not escaped either.

- Source citations: ApiTaskServlet.java:51-54 (.replace("\\","\\\\").replace("\"","\\\""))

- Actors and preconditions: Any client; a task whose title (or other exposed string) contains \n, tab, or < — achievable through the form POST because the servlet does not restrict content (TaskFormServlet.java:41-48).

- Trigger: POST /tasks/form with title containing a newline, then GET /api/tasks.

- Effects or refusal: Raw newline inside a JSON string token -> response is not parseable JSON (defect candidate D-003/escaping gap). Quote and backslash round-trip correctly.

- Observed versus intended: OBSERVED: quote/backslash safe, control chars/HTML not. INTENDED: spec-level JSON correctness. Defect candidate — reproducible case in findings/ledger.json.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-API-005

R-API-006

- ID: R-API-006

- Statement: /api/tasks accepts GET only: the servlet overrides doGet exclusively, so any other method (e.g. POST) falls through to the HttpServlet default and is refused with HTTP 405 Method Not Allowed; no state change.

- Source citations: ApiTaskServlet.java:18-23 (only doGet overridden); javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet default method handling; web.xml servlet-mapping apiTasks -> /api/tasks

- Actors and preconditions: Any client, authenticated or not (the filter exempts /api/).

- Trigger: POST (or other non-GET method) /api/tasks.

- Effects or refusal: HTTP 405 Method Not Allowed; no DB write.

- Observed versus intended: Observed (executed engineer evidence). Read-only API by design per source comment.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-API-008

rules/AUTH.md

Raw file

AUTH — authentication / session / logout / access exemptions

R-AUTH-001

- ID: R-AUTH-001

- Statement: Every request passes through AuthFilter (mapped to /*); requests to exactly /login, anything under /api/, or ending .css bypass the session check entirely.

- Source citations: web.xml:20-23; AuthFilter.java:25-31

- Actors and preconditions: Any HTTP client, with or without a session.

- Trigger: Request to any path.

- Effects or refusal: Exempt paths proceed through the filter chain even when unauthenticated; all other paths require a session.

- Observed versus intended: Observed. /api/* exemption makes the whole task readback public — noted as intended-by-comment (ApiTaskServlet.java:14-17) but security-relevant.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-AUTH-001, S-AUTH-002

R-AUTH-002

- ID: R-AUTH-002

- Statement: A request to a non-exempt path with no session or no user session attribute is redirected (302) to <ctx>/login.

- Source citations: AuthFilter.java:33-38

- Actors and preconditions: Unauthenticated client; any path other than the R-AUTH-001 exemptions (e.g. /tasks, /projects, /logout, /tasks/form, /tasks/action).

- Trigger: getSession(false) returns null or the user attribute is absent.

- Effects or refusal: HTTP 302 to /login; no servlet runs; no mutation.

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-AUTH-003

R-AUTH-003

- ID: R-AUTH-003

- Statement: POST /login with username/password matching a person row (plaintext comparison inside the SQL WHERE clause) stores the Person in a new session as user and redirects to /tasks.

- Source citations: LoginServlet.java:23-35; PersonDao.java:16-29

- Actors and preconditions: Any client; credentials for a seeded user (admin/admin, mgr/mgr, dev/dev, view/view — seed.sql:44-48).

- Trigger: Valid POST /login credentials.

- Effects or refusal: HTTP 302 to <ctx>/tasks; session attribute user set (getSession(true)); no DB write.

- Observed versus intended: Observed. Plaintext password comparison (PersonDao.java:18) is a legacy security smell (defect candidate D-007).

- Linked EARS IDs: S-AUTH-004

R-AUTH-004

- ID: R-AUTH-004

- Statement: POST /login with unknown/invalid credentials re-renders login.jsp with error message "Invalid credentials" at HTTP 200.

- Source citations: LoginServlet.java:28-31; login.jsp:20

- Actors and preconditions: Any client.

- Trigger: people.authenticate(...) returns null.

- Effects or refusal: No session created; no redirect; error paragraph rendered.

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-AUTH-005

R-AUTH-005

- ID: R-AUTH-005

- Statement: GET /login forwards to login.jsp (username/password form posting back to /login).

- Source citations: LoginServlet.java:17-20; login.jsp:22-32

- Actors and preconditions: Any client.

- Trigger: GET /login.

- Effects or refusal: Login form rendered; no mutation.

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-AUTH-006

R-AUTH-006

- ID: R-AUTH-006

- Statement: GET /logout invalidates the existing session (if any) and redirects to /login.

- Source citations: LogoutServlet.java:12-18

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated (or anonymous) client; must pass AuthFilter (not exempt).

- Trigger: GET /logout.

- Effects or refusal: Session invalidated; subsequent access to protected paths redirects to /login; no DB write.

- Observed versus intended: Observed. GET-based logout is a CSRF/logout-trick candidate (defect D-008).

- Linked EARS IDs: S-AUTH-007

R-AUTH-007

- ID: R-AUTH-007

- Statement: Only ADMIN or MANAGER may perform approve or reject on /tasks/action; other roles get HTTP 403. Enforced twice: in AuthFilter and again in TaskActionServlet.requireApprover.

- Source citations: AuthFilter.java:40-46; TaskActionServlet.java:91-98; Role.java:12-14

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated session user with role MEMBER or VIEWER (or a null user reaching the servlet directly).

- Trigger: POST /tasks/action with action=approve or action=reject.

- Effects or refusal: HTTP 403 "Only managers or admins may approve or reject tasks" (filter) / "Approval requires a manager or admin" (servlet); no status change.

- Observed versus intended: Observed. This is the ONLY role-gated action in the app — do not generalize it to other paths.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-AUTH-008, S-LIFE-005, S-LIFE-006

R-AUTH-008

- ID: R-AUTH-008

- Statement: There is no role restriction on any action other than approve/reject: task create/edit, start/submit/reopen, bulkClose, project create/archive are all available to every authenticated role including VIEWER.

- Source citations: ProjectServlet.java:31-51 (no role checks); TaskFormServlet.java:38-75 (no role checks); TaskActionServlet.java:23-74 (only requireApprover gates approve/reject); AuthFilter.java:40-46 (only approve/reject gate)

- Actors and preconditions: Any authenticated user of any role.

- Trigger: Any mutation POST listed above.

- Effects or refusal: Action executes normally; no refusal based on role.

- Observed versus intended: Observed. Explicitly do NOT infer a read-only policy for VIEWER from its name or from the approval policy — cite this rule instead.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-AUTH-009, S-BULK-004

R-AUTH-009

- ID: R-AUTH-009

- Statement: The authenticated pages display the session user's full name and role in the top nav, plus a logout link.

- Source citations: _header.jspf:49-53

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user viewing any authenticated JSP.

- Trigger: Page render.

- Effects or refusal: ${sessionScope.user.fullName} (${sessionScope.user.role}) and Logout link rendered.

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-AUTH-010

rules/BULK.md

Raw file

BULK — bulk close (empty / selected / mixed states / roles)

R-BULK-001

- ID: R-BULK-001

- Statement: POST /tasks/action with action=bulkClose and no ids (absent or zero values) redirects to /tasks with NO mutation.

- Source citations: TaskActionServlet.java:26-29,76-81; TaskDao.java:129-131

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user, any role.

- Trigger: POST action=bulkClose (no ids) or ids= empty.

- Effects or refusal: 302 /tasks; zero rows written (no-mutation case).

- Observed versus intended: Observed. Silent success gives the user no feedback that nothing happened.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-BULK-001

R-BULK-002

- ID: R-BULK-002

- Statement: bulkClose runs a single UPDATE setting status='DONE' and updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP for the selected ids WHERE status <> 'DONE'; non-numeric ids throw NumberFormatException -> HTTP 500 before any update.

- Source citations: TaskActionServlet.java:82-86; TaskDao.java:128-147

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user; selected task ids.

- Trigger: POST action=bulkClose&ids=1&ids=2.

- Effects or refusal: All selected OPEN/IN_PROGRESS/PENDING_APPROVAL tasks -> DONE; 302 /tasks; ids=abc -> 500 with no changes.

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-BULK-002

R-BULK-003

- ID: R-BULK-003

- Statement: Mixed-state selection: already-DONE ids in the selection are silently skipped (WHERE status <> 'DONE'); the response does not report how many rows were actually closed.

- Source citations: TaskDao.java:136-137; TaskActionServlet.java:86-87 (updateCount ignored)

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user; selection containing e.g. tasks 3 (PENDING_APPROVAL) and 4 (DONE).

- Trigger: POST action=bulkClose&ids=3&ids=4.

- Effects or refusal: Task 3 -> DONE; task 4 stays DONE untouched; no count surfaced anywhere.

- Observed versus intended: OBSERVED: silent partial effect. INTENDED: unstated; flagged as feedback gap (defect candidate D-006).

- Linked EARS IDs: S-BULK-003

R-BULK-004

- ID: R-BULK-004

- Statement: bulkClose has NO role gate: any authenticated role (MEMBER, VIEWER) can close PENDING_APPROVAL tasks through it, bypassing the approval policy enforced on single-task approve/reject (403 there).

- Source citations: TaskActionServlet.java:23-29 (dispatch before requireApprover; no role check in handleBulkClose 76-88); AuthFilter.java:40-46 (filter gates only approve/reject); TaskDao.java:136-137; contrast TaskActionServlet.java:91-98

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated MEMBER or VIEWER; task 3 seeded PENDING_APPROVAL.

- Trigger: POST action=bulkClose&ids=3 as dev (MEMBER).

- Effects or refusal: Task 3 -> DONE, 302; NO 403. Same POST as action=approve would 403.

- Observed versus intended: OBSERVED: approval policy bypassed on the bulk path. INTENDED: approval policy (README.md domain table: "Only ADMIN/MANAGER may approve or reject tasks awaiting approval"). Defect candidate D-002 — reproducible case supplied.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-BULK-004, S-AUTH-009

R-BULK-005

- ID: R-BULK-005

- Statement: The list UI exposes bulkClose as one form wrapping the whole table with a checkbox (name=ids) per row and a "Close selected (bulk)" submit; no per-row confirmation, selection survives no state.

- Source citations: tasks.jsp:38-39 (hidden action=bulkClose), tasks.jsp:47 (checkbox), tasks.jsp:75-78 (submit button)

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user viewing /tasks.

- Trigger: Check rows, submit the bulk form.

- Effects or refusal: POST as in R-BULK-002.

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-BULK-005

rules/DATA.md

Raw file

DATA — database constraints / DAO mappings / technical & unreachable branches

R-DATA-001

- ID: R-DATA-001

- Statement: The schema (three tables, identity PKs) is owned by the Postgres container init, not the app: the app only waits for connectivity (30 x 1s retries, then RuntimeException).

- Source citations: schema.sql:1-7; Database.java:12-14,42-59; BootstrapListener.java:10-13

- Actors and preconditions: Container orchestration; app startup.

- Trigger: contextInitialized.

- Effects or refusal: App refuses to start if DB unreachable after ~30s; never creates or migrates tables.

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-DATA-001

R-DATA-002

- ID: R-DATA-002

- Statement: person: username VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL UNIQUE, full_name VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL, password VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL, role CHECK in (ADMIN,MANAGER,MEMBER,VIEWER). Seeded: admin/mgr/dev/view with passwords equal to usernames.

- Source citations: schema.sql:9-16; seed.sql:44-48; Role.java:7-11

- Actors and preconditions: DB init.

- Trigger: Any person write outside these bounds.

- Effects or refusal: Constraint violation -> SQLException -> RuntimeException -> HTTP 500 via the calling servlet.

- Observed versus intended: Observed. Passwords stored/compared in plaintext (PersonDao.java:18) — legacy smell, defect D-008.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-DATA-002, S-AUTH-004

R-DATA-003

- ID: R-DATA-003

- Statement: project: name VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL, description VARCHAR(1024) NULL, state NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE' CHECK (ACTIVE,ARCHIVED), owner_id NOT NULL FK -> person. The servlet forces 'ACTIVE' on create; only archive() writes ARCHIVED (forward-only; no code path back to ACTIVE).

- Source citations: schema.sql:18-26; ProjectDao.java:57-72 (create), 75-84 (archive)

- Actors and preconditions: DB + app writes.

- Trigger: Project create/archive.

- Effects or refusal: State machine enforced by WHERE state='ACTIVE'; invalid values only via direct DB (no web path).

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-DATA-003, S-PROJ-002

R-DATA-004

- ID: R-DATA-004

- Statement: task: title VARCHAR(256) NOT NULL, description VARCHAR(4000) NULL, status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'OPEN' CHECK (OPEN,IN_PROGRESS,PENDING_APPROVAL,DONE), priority NOT NULL DEFAULT 'MEDIUM' CHECK (LOW,MEDIUM,HIGH), assignee_id NULL FK -> person, project_id NOT NULL FK -> project, due_date DATE NULL, updated_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP.

- Source citations: schema.sql:28-42

- Actors and preconditions: DB + app writes.

- Trigger: Any task insert/update violating width/CHECK/FK/NOT NULL.

- Effects or refusal: SQLException -> RuntimeException -> HTTP 500; no partial write (single statements).

- Observed versus intended: Observed. The app relies on these as the last line of defense (see R-FORM-005/007/008 500-paths).

- Linked EARS IDs: S-DATA-004, S-FORM-005, S-FORM-010

R-DATA-005

- ID: R-DATA-005

- Statement: Task read mapping joins project (INNER) and assignee (LEFT): assigneeName/projectName are transient display fields; assignee_id uses getLong+wasNull for null; due_date -> ISO string or null; updated_at is truncated to 19 chars (seconds) before display/API.

- Source citations: TaskDao.java:15-20 (BASE), 220-238 (map: 227-228 wasNull, 232-233 date, 234-236 timestamp trim)

- Actors and preconditions: Any task read path.

- Trigger: findAll/findById/search.

- Effects or refusal: NULL assignee/due preserved as Java null; updatedAt loses sub-second precision.

- Observed versus intended: Observed. substring(0,19) assumes full timestamp format (always true for JDBC Timestamp).

- Linked EARS IDs: S-DATA-005, S-API-004

R-DATA-006

- ID: R-DATA-006

- Statement: Task writes: create INSERTs with status as provided (web layer always OPEN) and returns generated keys; update rewrites title/description/priority/assignee_id/due_date and sets updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP but never touches status; updateStatus writes status+updated_at only; bulkClose is one UPDATE with an IN-list and WHERE status <> 'DONE'. Nullable values written via setNull (BIGINT/DATE).

- Source citations: TaskDao.java:75-95 (create), 97-112 (update), 114-125 (updateStatus), 128-147 (bulkClose), 174-188 (setNullableLong/setNullableDate)

- Actors and preconditions: Web layer via TaskDao.

- Trigger: Any task mutation.

- Effects or refusal: updated_at bumped on every write path; status and fields are disjoint per method.

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-DATA-006, S-FORM-009, S-BULK-002

R-DATA-007

- ID: R-DATA-007

- Statement: Technical/unreachable branches: TaskDao.findByProject and ProjectDao.findById have no caller in the web layer; Database.awaitReady interruption path returns silently; oracle_ro is a SELECT-only DB role recreated per compose-up (no app path); LifecycleTest drives updateStatus directly, bypassing servlet guards (OPEN->DONE succeeds at DAO level).

- Source citations: TaskDao.java:31-33; ProjectDao.java:33-43; Database.java:50-55; oracle-ro.sql:64-75; src/test/java/com/acme/tracker/LifecycleTest.java:27-55

- Actors and preconditions: Code readers / DB admins; no HTTP surface.

- Trigger: n/a (inventory coverage of technical paths).

- Effects or refusal: No user-observable behavior; guards are presentation-layer only.

- Observed versus intended: Observed — recorded so the engineer does not treat LifecycleTest's unguarded transition as a web-accessible rule.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-DATA-007

R-DATA-008

- ID: R-DATA-008

- Statement: JSP-observable output contract: authenticated pages share _header.jspf (nav, user identity, logout); statuses carry badge classes status-<STATUS>; the form page shows "(unassigned)" for NULL assignee and "—" for NULL due date; the "Saved." banner keys on param saved=1; footer version string v1.0.

- Source citations: _header.jspf:43-53; task-form.jsp:9-16 (badge css), 29 (saved), 37,39 (null placeholders); tasks.jsp/footer lines 89; login.jsp:35

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated rendering.

- Trigger: Page requests.

- Effects or refusal: Pure view output; no mutation.

- Observed versus intended: Observed. Note: EL ${...} is not HTML-escaped — stored-XSS candidate D-004 for titles containing markup.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-DATA-008, S-QUERY-008, S-FORM-008

FORM — task form read / create / edit / validation / defaults / nullable fields

R-FORM-001

- ID: R-FORM-001

- Statement: GET /tasks/form with no id renders a blank "New task" form; with id it renders the task detail panel plus a pre-filled edit form; projects and people selects are always populated.

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user.

- Trigger: GET /tasks/form[?id=n].

- Effects or refusal: HTTP 200; no mutation.

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-FORM-001

R-FORM-002

- ID: R-FORM-002

- Statement: GET /tasks/form?id=<unknown> silently renders the "New task" form (the task attribute is null, so the JSP takes the new-task branch) instead of a 404; id=abc throws NumberFormatException -> HTTP 500.

- Source citations: TaskFormServlet.java:29-31 (Long.parseLong); task-form.jsp:27 (c:otherwise New task)

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user.

- Trigger: GET /tasks/form?id=999 (no such row) / id=abc.

- Effects or refusal: 200 "New task" (misleading) / HTTP 500 respectively; no mutation.

- Observed versus intended: OBSERVED: not-found is silently mislabeled as "New task". INTENDED: a not-found signal. Defect candidate D-003.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-FORM-002

R-FORM-003

- ID: R-FORM-003

- Statement: POST /tasks/form requires a non-blank title (trimmed); blank/null -> re-render form with error "Title is required" at HTTP 200, nothing persisted.

- Source citations: TaskFormServlet.java:41-48,77-87

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user.

- Trigger: POST with title="" or missing.

- Effects or refusal: 200 with error attr; no DB write; form re-populated with project/person selects.

- Observed versus intended: Observed (mirrors task.title NOT NULL, schema.sql:30).

- Linked EARS IDs: S-FORM-003

R-FORM-004

- ID: R-FORM-004

- Statement: POST /tasks/form requires projectId to be present and to exist; violation -> re-render with "A task must belong to an existing project"; existence is state-blind, so ARCHIVED projects are accepted; non-numeric projectId -> HTTP 500.

- Source citations: TaskFormServlet.java:50-53; ProjectDao.java:45-55

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user.

- Trigger: POST with projectId missing/empty/999/abc.

- Effects or refusal: 200 error render (missing/unknown), 500 (non-numeric); no DB write.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-FORM-004

R-FORM-005

- ID: R-FORM-005

- Statement: priority is defaulted to MEDIUM when absent or empty, but any other arbitrary value passes the servlet unchecked and only fails at the DB CHECK constraint -> RuntimeException -> HTTP 500.

- Source citations: TaskFormServlet.java:58,89-91; TaskDao.java:83; schema.sql:34-35

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user (crafted POST; the HTML form only offers LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH — task-form.jsp:96-103).

- Trigger: POST priority=URGENT with valid title/projectId.

- Effects or refusal: HTTP 500; no row persisted (INSERT fails).

- Observed versus intended: OBSERVED: server-side validation gap between form whitelist and DB CHECK. INTENDED: presumably a 400-style refusal. Defect candidate D-004.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-FORM-005

R-FORM-006

- Statement: assigneeId is nullable: absent/empty -> SQL NULL (unassigned); a non-numeric value -> HTTP 500; a value not present in person -> FK violation -> HTTP 500.

R-FORM-008

- ID: R-FORM-008

- Statement: POST with no id creates a task with status forced to OPEN and returns the generated id; POST with id updates title/description/priority/assignee/dueDate and NEVER changes status, and bumps updated_at; both land on /tasks/form?id=<id>&saved=1.

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user; existing task for the edit case.

- Trigger: POST /tasks/form.

- Effects or refusal: Task persisted; 302 to form with saved=1; JSP shows "Saved." banner (task-form.jsp:29).

- Observed versus intended: Observed. Title/description > column widths (256/4000) hit DB limits -> 500 (boundary, schema.sql:30-31).

- Linked EARS IDs: S-FORM-008, S-FORM-009, S-FORM-010

rules/LIFE.md

Raw file

LIFE — task lifecycle: start / submit / approve / reject / reopen, state guards, not-found, unknown actions

R-LIFE-001

- ID: R-LIFE-001

- Statement: POST /tasks/action with action=start on a task whose current status is OPEN sets status IN_PROGRESS and bumps updated_at; any other current status is refused with HTTP 409 naming both statuses.

- Source citations: TaskActionServlet.java:40-43,101-109; TaskDao.java:115-125

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user (any role); existing task id.

- Trigger: POST action=start&id=<n>.

- Effects or refusal: OPEN -> IN_PROGRESS with updated_at refresh, 302 /tasks (or /tasks/form?id=n when return=detail, TaskActionServlet.java:66-73); otherwise HTTP 409 "Cannot start a task in status <current> (expected OPEN)" and no write.

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-LIFE-001

R-LIFE-002

- ID: R-LIFE-002

- Statement: action=submit requires current IN_PROGRESS -> PENDING_APPROVAL; refusal otherwise (409).

- Source citations: TaskActionServlet.java:44-47

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user, any role (no gate on submit).

- Trigger: POST action=submit&id=<n>.

- Effects or refusal: IN_PROGRESS -> PENDING_APPROVAL (updated_at refreshed) or 409 "Cannot submit ... (expected IN_PROGRESS)".

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-LIFE-002

R-LIFE-003

- ID: R-LIFE-003

- Statement: action=approve requires an ADMIN/MANAGER user (403 otherwise) AND current status PENDING_APPROVAL (409 otherwise); on success status -> DONE.

- Source citations: TaskActionServlet.java:48-52,91-98; AuthFilter.java:40-46; Role.java:12-14

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user; role checked before guard; filter also rejects early.

- Trigger: POST action=approve&id=<n>.

- Effects or refusal: PENDING_APPROVAL -> DONE / 403 (MEMBER, VIEWER) / 409 (wrong status).

- Observed versus intended: Observed. Approval policy applies ONLY here and to reject — not to bulkClose or any other path.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-LIFE-005

R-LIFE-004

- ID: R-LIFE-004

- Statement: action=reject has the same role and status requirements as approve but sends the task back to IN_PROGRESS.

- Source citations: TaskActionServlet.java:53-57

- Actors and preconditions: Same as R-LIFE-003.

- Trigger: POST action=reject&id=<n>.

- Effects or refusal: PENDING_APPROVAL -> IN_PROGRESS / 403 / 409.

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-LIFE-006

R-LIFE-005

- ID: R-LIFE-005

- Statement: action=reopen requires current DONE -> OPEN (the only backward transition); refusal otherwise (409).

- Source citations: TaskActionServlet.java:58-61

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user, any role.

- Trigger: POST action=reopen&id=<n>.

- Effects or refusal: DONE -> OPEN / 409 "Cannot reopen ... (expected DONE)".

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-LIFE-007

R-LIFE-006

- ID: R-LIFE-006

- Statement: Any lifecycle action for an unknown task id returns HTTP 404 "No such task"; a non-numeric id returns HTTP 500; a missing/unknown action returns HTTP 400 ("Unknown action: <value>", value rendered as the string null when the param is absent).

- Source citations: TaskActionServlet.java:31-36,62-64

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user.

- Trigger: POST with id=999 / id=abc / action=teleport / no action param.

- Effects or refusal: 404 / 500 / 400 / 400 respectively; no writes.

- Observed versus intended: Observed. Unknown action echo is unescaped into the 400 body (minor).

- Linked EARS IDs: S-LIFE-003, S-LIFE-004

R-LIFE-007

- ID: R-LIFE-007

- Statement: After a successful single-task action the servlet redirects to /tasks by default, or /tasks/form?id=<n> when return=detail (the detail-view transition buttons).

- Source citations: TaskActionServlet.java:66-73; task-form.jsp:43-59 (buttons carry hidden return=detail); tasks.jsp:57-69 (list buttons use per-row forms without return)

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user; successful guard pass.

- Trigger: POST action=... [&return=detail].

- Effects or refusal: 302 to the chosen target; no body.

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-LIFE-008

R-LIFE-008

- ID: R-LIFE-008

- Statement: The UI renders only the buttons valid for the row's current status (start when OPEN, submit when IN_PROGRESS, approve+reject when PENDING_APPROVAL, reopen when DONE) in both list and detail views — the JSP mirrors of the servlet guards.

- Source citations: tasks.jsp:57-69; task-form.jsp:46-58; schema.sql:32-33 (authoritative status values); TaskStatus.java:9-13

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user viewing tasks.jsp or task-form.jsp.

- Trigger: Page render.

- Effects or refusal: Exactly one transition group visible per row; direct POSTs can still attempt any action (server-side guards are the authority).

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-LIFE-009

R-LIFE-009

- ID: R-LIFE-009

- Statement: At DAO level updateStatus writes any status unconditionally (raw write, no guard) — the existing LifecycleTest uses this to jump OPEN->DONE directly; the servlet guards exist only in the web layer.

- Source citations: TaskDao.java:114-125; src/test/java/com/acme/tracker/LifecycleTest.java:27-55

- Actors and preconditions: Java caller of TaskDao (no HTTP path).

- Trigger: tasks.updateStatus(id, DONE) on an OPEN task.

- Effects or refusal: Status changes with no refusal; demonstrates guards are presentation-layer only.

- Observed versus intended: Observed; inventory note, not a defect.

- Linked EARS IDs: (none — technical observation, no web-observable spec)

R-LIFE-010

- ID: R-LIFE-010

- Statement: /tasks/action accepts POST only: the servlet overrides doPost exclusively, so a GET falls through to the HttpServlet default and is refused with HTTP 405 Method Not Allowed; no state change and no action dispatch.

- Source citations: TaskActionServlet.java:18-23 (only doPost overridden); javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet default method handling; web.xml servlet-mapping taskAction -> /tasks/action

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user (AuthFilter maps /*); any role.

- Trigger: GET /tasks/action.

- Effects or refusal: HTTP 405 Method Not Allowed; no DB write, no redirect.

- Observed versus intended: Observed (executed engineer evidence). Mutations are POST-only by construction.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-LIFE-010

rules/PROJ.md

PROJ — projects list / create / archive / validation

R-PROJ-001

- ID: R-PROJ-001

- Statement: GET /projects lists ALL projects (ACTIVE and ARCHIVED) ordered by name, joined with owner full name, and forwards projects.jsp.

- Source citations: ProjectServlet.java:23-28; ProjectDao.java:19-31 (ORDER BY p.name); projects.jsp:135-156

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user.

- Trigger: GET /projects.

- Effects or refusal: HTTP 200 table with id/name/description/state/owner; no mutation.

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-PROJ-001

R-PROJ-002

- ID: R-PROJ-002

- Statement: POST /projects with action=archive&id=<n> sets state='ARCHIVED' only where state is currently 'ACTIVE'; for an unknown id or an already-ARCHIVED project the UPDATE matches zero rows and the servlet still redirects with no error.

- Source citations: ProjectServlet.java:33-37; ProjectDao.java:75-84

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user (any role — no gate, see R-AUTH-008).

- Trigger: POST /projects action=archive.

- Effects or refusal: project.state -> ARCHIVED (forward-only; no unarchive path exists anywhere in source); 302 /projects; silent no-op when target is not ACTIVE.

- Observed versus intended: Observed. Silent no-op on unknown id is observed behavior; intended behavior is unstated in source — flagged as a gap.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-PROJ-002, S-PROJ-003

R-PROJ-003

- ID: R-PROJ-003

- Statement: POST /projects archive with a missing or non-numeric id throws NumberFormatException -> HTTP 500.

- Source citations: ProjectServlet.java:34 (Long.parseLong(req.getParameter("id")))

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user.

- Trigger: POST /projects action=archive without id, or id=abc.

- Effects or refusal: HTTP 500; no state change.

- Observed versus intended: Observed; refusal path is unhandled (defect candidate D-005 family).

- Linked EARS IDs: S-PROJ-004

R-PROJ-004

- ID: R-PROJ-004

- Statement: POST /projects (no action param) creates a project: name required, blank/null after trim -> HTTP 400 "Project name is required"; otherwise name is trimmed and inserted with state 'ACTIVE' and ownerId = session user's id; 302 /projects on success.

- Source citations: ProjectServlet.java:38-50; ProjectDao.java:57-72 (VALUES (?, ?, 'ACTIVE', ?))

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user of any role.

- Trigger: POST /projects with name.

- Effects or refusal: New project row (state ACTIVE, owner = current user); 302 /projects; HTTP 400 refusal on blank name.

- Observed versus intended: Observed. Note no name-length pre-check; a name > 128 chars hits the DB VARCHAR(128) and surfaces as HTTP 500 (schema.sql:20).

- Linked EARS IDs: S-PROJ-005, S-PROJ-006

R-PROJ-005

- ID: R-PROJ-005

- Statement: Project description is stored as-submitted: absent -> NULL, blank -> empty string; it is never trimmed and never length-checked in the servlet.

- Source citations: ProjectServlet.java:47; ProjectDao.java:61-62; schema.sql:21 (VARCHAR(1024) NULL)

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user.

- Trigger: POST /projects with/without description.

- Effects or refusal: NULL vs '' observable in projects.jsp description column.

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-PROJ-006

R-PROJ-006

- ID: R-PROJ-006

- Statement: projects.jsp renders the Archive button only for ACTIVE projects and always renders a "Delete" link targeting /projects/delete?id=<n> — but no servlet is mapped to /projects/delete, so the link yields HTTP 404.

- Source citations: projects.jsp:145-151 (Archive), projects.jsp:152 (Delete link); web.xml:71-78 (only /projects is mapped)

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user viewing /projects.

- Trigger: Clicking Delete (GET /projects/delete).

- Effects or refusal: HTTP 404 from the container; no mutation.

- Observed versus intended: OBSERVED: dead affordance returning 404. INTENDED: presumably deletion or no link. Defect candidate D-001 with reproducible case.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-PROJ-007

R-PROJ-007

- ID: R-PROJ-007

- Statement: Archiving a project does NOT restrict any task operation on it: the task form validates project existence only (exists matches any id regardless of state), so tasks can be created in and edited while belonging to an ARCHIVED project.

- Source citations: TaskFormServlet.java:50-53; ProjectDao.java:45-55 (SELECT 1 FROM project WHERE id = ? — no state predicate); schema.sql:22-23 (state values)

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user; project 3 'Legacy Archive' is seeded ARCHIVED (seed.sql:54).

- Trigger: POST /tasks/form with projectId=3.

- Effects or refusal: Task created/updated normally; no refusal.

- Observed versus intended: OBSERVED: archived projects still accept tasks. INTENDED: unstated in source; flagged as defect candidate D-002 (observed-vs-intended gap for the engineer to confirm).

- Linked EARS IDs: S-PROJ-008, S-FORM-004

rules/QUERY.md

Raw file

QUERY — task list status/assignee filters and every whitelisted/default sort

R-QUERY-001

- ID: R-QUERY-001

- Statement: GET /tasks with no filter params returns ALL tasks (filters are optional; empty WHERE). Filtering is centralized through TaskDao.search even for the default list.

- Source citations: TaskListServlet.java:24-32 (comment lines 28-30); TaskDao.java:40-56

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user.

- Trigger: GET /tasks.

- Effects or refusal: HTTP 200 with every task row; default sort applied.

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-QUERY-001

R-QUERY-002

- ID: R-QUERY-002

- Statement: status filter, when non-empty, becomes an exact-match parameterized predicate t.status = ?; a value that is not one of the four statuses yields an EMPTY list (HTTP 200) — no whitelist check and no error; empty string means no filter.

- Source citations: TaskListServlet.java:24; TaskDao.java:44-47; tasks.jsp:18-23 (UI options: all + OPEN/IN_PROGRESS/PENDING_APPROVAL/DONE)

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user.

- Trigger: GET /tasks?status=DONE, /tasks?status=, /tasks?status=BOGUS.

- Effects or refusal: DONE-only list / all tasks / empty list respectively; no mutation ever.

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-QUERY-002

R-QUERY-003

- ID: R-QUERY-003

- Statement: assignee filter, when non-empty, becomes parameterized t.assignee_id = ?; non-numeric value -> NumberFormatException -> HTTP 500; empty string means no filter. The filter is on assignee id, not name.

- Source citations: TaskListServlet.java:25,31; TaskDao.java:48-51

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user.

- Trigger: GET /tasks?assignee=3, /tasks?assignee=, /tasks?assignee=abc.

- Effects or refusal: Tasks assigned to person 3 / all tasks / 500 respectively.

- Observed versus intended: Observed (500 on bad input, defect candidate D-005 family).

- Linked EARS IDs: S-QUERY-003

R-QUERY-004

- ID: R-QUERY-004

- Statement: sort is a UI token whitelisted in TaskDao.orderClause; combined filters AND together. Tokens: updated (default) updated_at DESC; status status then updated_at DESC; priority semantic HIGH>MEDIUM>LOW via CASE (NOT alphabetical) then updated_at DESC; due and due_date due_date then updated_at DESC (NULLs sort per DB ordering); title title then id; id id. Any other token silently falls back to the default.

- Source citations: TaskDao.java:56,154-172 (each branch cited: 155-157, 158-161, 162-164, 165-167, 168-170, 171); TaskListServlet.java:36 (sortBy attr)

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user.

- Trigger: GET /tasks?sort=<token>, alone or combined with status/assignee.

- Effects or refusal: Deterministic ordering per token; no mutation; unknown token = default order, no error.

- Observed versus intended: Observed. Sort token never concatenated from user input (injection-safe by whitelist, TaskDao.java:55-56).

- Linked EARS IDs: S-QUERY-004, S-QUERY-005, S-QUERY-006, S-QUERY-007

R-QUERY-005

- ID: R-QUERY-005

- Statement: The JSP renders the filter/sort toolbar with auto-submit selects: status options (all) + the four statuses; sort options exactly updated/status/priority/due/title/id; the selected option mirrors the served sortBy attribute (raw sort param echoed into the comparison only).

- Source citations: tasks.jsp:15-35; TaskListServlet.java:35-36

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user.

- Trigger: GET /tasks render.

- Effects or refusal: Toolbar state reflects current query; no mutation.

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-QUERY-008

R-QUERY-006

- ID: R-QUERY-006

- Statement: The task list table renders per row: checkbox, id, linked title (link to /tasks/form?id=), project name, assignee name, status, priority, due date, updatedAt, and transition buttons; assignee/due render blank when NULL (display join via LEFT JOIN person).

- Source citations: tasks.jsp:40-72; TaskDao.java:15-20 (LEFT JOIN), 227-233 (null mapping)

- Actors and preconditions: Authenticated user.

- Trigger: List render.

- Effects or refusal: Observable view; NULL assignee_id/due_date show as empty cells (task 4 has no due date).

- Observed versus intended: Observed.

- Linked EARS IDs: S-QUERY-001, S-QUERY-003

specs/API.md

Raw file

API — specifications

S-API-001

- ID: S-API-001

- EARS statement: WHEN GET /api/tasks is requested without any session, THE SYSTEM SHALL return HTTP 200 application/json with the task array (access-exemption case).

- Linked rule IDs: R-API-001

- Fixtures and inputs: Fresh anonymous client; GET /api/tasks.

- Observable assertions: 200; Content-Type application/json; body is a JSON array of 4 seeded tasks.

- Executable test IDs: T-API-001 (planned)

S-API-002

- ID: S-API-002

- EARS statement: WHEN GET /api/tasks?status=<valid status>, THE SYSTEM SHALL return only matching tasks; WHEN status is empty or absent, all tasks; WHEN status is unknown, an empty array with HTTP 200; assignee/sort params are ignored.

- Linked rule IDs: R-API-002

- Fixtures and inputs: status=DONE; status=; status=BOGUS; assignee=3&sort=title.

- Observable assertions: [task 4] / [4 tasks] / [] / [4 tasks].

- Executable test IDs: T-API-002 (planned)

S-API-003

- ID: S-API-003

- EARS statement: WHEN the API returns a task, THE SYSTEM SHALL include exactly the fields id,title,status,priority,assigneeId,projectId,dueDate,updatedAt and no description.

- Linked rule IDs: R-API-003

- Fixtures and inputs: GET /api/tasks.

- Observable assertions: Every object has exactly those 8 keys; updatedAt matches /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2} \d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2}$/.

- Executable test IDs: T-API-003 (planned)

S-API-004

- ID: S-API-004

- EARS statement: WHEN a task has NULL assignee_id or due_date, THE SYSTEM SHALL emit JSON null for assigneeId/dueDate (null-handling case).

- Linked rule IDs: R-API-004

- Fixtures and inputs: GET /api/tasks (seed task 4: assignee 1... actually assignee_id=1, due_date NULL — for a NULL assignee, unassign task 4 via the form first).

- Observable assertions: Task 4 object contains "dueDate":null; after unassigning, "assigneeId":null; body parses as JSON.

- Executable test IDs: T-API-004 (planned)

S-API-005

- ID: S-API-005

- EARS statement: WHEN an exposed string contains a double quote or backslash, THE SYSTEM SHALL escape it and the body stays valid JSON; WHEN it contains a newline or control char, THE SYSTEM SHALL emit it raw and the body ceases to be valid JSON (observed defect, escaping boundary).

- Linked rule IDs: R-API-005

- Fixtures and inputs: POST /tasks/form title=Quote"Test&projectId=1 -> GET /api/tasks parses; POST title=Line1\nLine2 (literal newline) -> GET /api/tasks fails JSON parse.

- Observable assertions: First body parses and title round-trips; second body contains a raw line break inside a string token.

- Executable test IDs: T-API-005 (planned)

S-API-006

- ID: S-API-006

- EARS statement: WHEN GET /api/tasks is requested without a status filter, THE SYSTEM SHALL return every task in the default order updated_at DESC (newest first), matching the UI list default.

- Linked rule IDs: R-API-002, R-QUERY-001

- Fixtures and inputs: GET /api/tasks (seeded tasks 1-4 with staggered updated_at 09:00,12:00,15:00,18:00 — seed.sql:59-63).

- Observable assertions: HTTP 200; JSON array ordered 4,3,2,1 by id.

- Executable test IDs: test_api.ApiReadbackTests.test_s_api_004_api_default_order_is_updated_desc (executed, engineer)

- Note: engineer provisional ID S-API-004 (their numbering) — no analyst spec previously stated the API default order. Intended-requirement spec.

S-API-007

- ID: S-API-007

- EARS statement: (characterization) WHEN an exposed string contains HTML-significant characters (<, >, &), THE SYSTEM SHALL emit them unescaped in the JSON body — esc() escapes only backslash and double-quote (ApiTaskServlet.java:51-54).

- Linked rule IDs: R-API-005

- Fixtures and inputs: Task title containing <b>&amp;</b>-style markup stored via POST /tasks/form; GET /api/tasks.

- Observable assertions: JSON body contains the raw characters; body still parses as JSON; readback equals the stored string.

- Executable test IDs: test_api.ApiEscapingAndNullTests.test_s_api_012_html_special_characters_pass_through_unescaped (executed, engineer; D-008 evidence)

- Intendedness disputed (run 05): recorded as observed behavior only; intendedness disputed under defect D-008 (quote/backslash-only escaping). The intended requirement (spec-correct JSON/HTML escaping) is deliberately NOT written here as if sourced. Engineer provisional ID S-API-012.

S-API-008

- ID: S-API-008

- EARS statement: WHEN POST /api/tasks is requested, THE SYSTEM SHALL respond HTTP 405 Method Not Allowed and write nothing (the endpoint implements GET only).

- Linked rule IDs: R-API-006

- Fixtures and inputs: POST /api/tasks (empty or arbitrary body) as any client.

- Observable assertions: HTTP 405; task row count unchanged.

- Executable test IDs: test_api.ApiEscapingAndNullTests.test_s_api_014_api_ignores_post_method (executed, engineer)

- Note: engineer provisional ID S-API-014 — no analyst spec previously stated API method handling. Intended-requirement spec.

AUTH — specifications

S-AUTH-001

- ID: S-AUTH-001

- EARS statement: WHEN a request targets /login, /api/*, or a .css path, THE SYSTEM SHALL forward it to the servlet without any session check.

- Linked rule IDs: R-AUTH-001

- Fixtures and inputs: Anonymous HTTP client; GET /login, GET /api/tasks, GET /styles.css.

- Observable assertions: HTTP 200 (or API JSON) returned; no 302 to /login; no Set-Cookie requirement.

- Executable test IDs: T-AUTH-001 (planned)

S-AUTH-002

- ID: S-AUTH-002

- EARS statement: WHEN an unauthenticated request targets any other path, THE SYSTEM SHALL respond 302 to the login page before any servlet logic runs.

- Linked rule IDs: R-AUTH-001, R-AUTH-002

- Fixtures and inputs: Anonymous client; GET /tasks, GET /projects, POST /tasks/action.

- Observable assertions: 302 with Location ending /login; no DB mutation occurs.

- Executable test IDs: T-AUTH-002 (planned)

S-AUTH-003

- ID: S-AUTH-003

- EARS statement: WHEN a session exists but lacks the user attribute, THE SYSTEM SHALL treat the request as unauthenticated and redirect to /login.

- Linked rule IDs: R-AUTH-002

- Fixtures and inputs: Fresh session (e.g. via GET /login) then GET /tasks.

- Observable assertions: 302 to /login.

- Executable test IDs: T-AUTH-003 (planned)

S-AUTH-004

- ID: S-AUTH-004

- EARS statement: WHEN POST /login carries credentials matching a person row, THE SYSTEM SHALL create a session with attribute user and redirect to /tasks.

- Linked rule IDs: R-AUTH-003

- Fixtures and inputs: POST username=dev, password=dev.

- Observable assertions: 302 to /tasks; subsequent GET /tasks returns 200 with "Devon Member" in the header.

- Executable test IDs: T-AUTH-004 (planned)

S-AUTH-005

- ID: S-AUTH-005

- Linked rule IDs: R-AUTH-004

S-AUTH-007

- Linked rule IDs: R-AUTH-006

- Fixtures and inputs: Authenticated session; then GET /logout; then GET /tasks. Repeat with no session.

- Observable assertions: 302 to /login in both cases; after logout GET /tasks is 302 to /login.

- Executable test IDs: T-AUTH-007 (planned)

S-AUTH-008

- ID: S-AUTH-008

- Linked rule IDs: R-AUTH-007

- Fixtures and inputs: Login as dev (MEMBER); task 3 is seeded PENDING_APPROVAL; POST /tasks/action id=3&action=approve.

- Observable assertions: HTTP 403; GET /api/tasks shows task 3 still PENDING_APPROVAL.

- Executable test IDs: T-AUTH-008 (planned)

S-AUTH-009

- ID: S-AUTH-009

- EARS statement: WHEN an authenticated VIEWER posts any non-approve/reject mutation (task create, bulkClose, project archive), THE SYSTEM SHALL execute it exactly as for any other role.

- Linked rule IDs: R-AUTH-008

- Fixtures and inputs: Login as view (VIEWER); POST /tasks/form with valid title+projectId=1.

- Executable test IDs: T-AUTH-009 (planned)

S-AUTH-010

- Linked rule IDs: R-AUTH-009

- Executable test IDs: T-AUTH-010 (planned)

BULK — specifications

S-BULK-001

- ID: S-BULK-001

- EARS statement: WHEN action=bulkClose is posted with no ids, THE SYSTEM SHALL redirect to /tasks without modifying any task.

- Linked rule IDs: R-BULK-001

- Fixtures and inputs: POST /tasks/action action=bulkClose.

- Observable assertions: 302 /tasks; API readback identical before/after.

- Executable test IDs: T-BULK-001 (planned)

S-BULK-002

- ID: S-BULK-002

- EARS statement: WHEN action=bulkClose is posted with selected ids, THE SYSTEM SHALL set all selected non-DONE tasks to DONE in one update and redirect; WHEN any id is non-numeric, THE SYSTEM SHALL respond HTTP 500 without changing anything.

- Linked rule IDs: R-BULK-002

- Fixtures and inputs: (a) ids=1&ids=2; (b) ids=1&ids=abc.

- Observable assertions: (a) 302; tasks 1,2 DONE in readback; (b) 500; both unchanged.

- Executable test IDs: T-BULK-002 (planned)

S-BULK-003

- ID: S-BULK-003

- EARS statement: WHEN the bulkClose selection mixes statuses including DONE, THE SYSTEM SHALL close the non-DONE rows and leave DONE rows untouched, reporting nothing (mixed-state boundary).

- Linked rule IDs: R-BULK-003

- Fixtures and inputs: ids=3&ids=4 (task 3 PENDING_APPROVAL, task 4 DONE).

- Observable assertions: 302; task 3 DONE, task 4 still DONE with unchanged updated_at.

- Executable test IDs: T-BULK-003 (planned)

S-BULK-004

- ID: S-BULK-004

- EARS statement: WHEN a MEMBER posts action=bulkClose containing a PENDING_APPROVAL task, THE SYSTEM SHALL close it without a 403 (observed approval bypass, defect D-002 — expected per approval policy: 403).

- Linked rule IDs: R-BULK-004, R-AUTH-008

- Fixtures and inputs: Login as dev (MEMBER); POST action=bulkClose&ids=3.

- Observable assertions: 302; task 3 DONE in readback; no 403 anywhere.

- Executable test IDs: T-BULK-004 (planned)

S-BULK-005

- ID: S-BULK-005

- EARS statement: WHEN the task list renders, THE SYSTEM SHALL provide one bulkClose form with per-row checkboxes (render-only, no-mutation case).

- Linked rule IDs: R-BULK-005

- Fixtures and inputs: GET /tasks.

tests/build_mapping.py

Raw file

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Generate reports/tests/mapping.json from the test suite docstrings.

Every test's docstring must begin with its provisional EARS id
(S-<FAMILY>-NNN); appending "[characterization]" marks an
observed-behavior case rather than an intended requirement. The generator
walks the discovered suite so the mapping can never drift from the tests.

Since dispatch e02 the provisional ids are reconciled to the accepted
analyst spec ids (refs/campaign/orchestrator, commit 6f55e3f). ACCEPTED
below maps each full test id to its accepted S-<FAMILY>-NNN id(s) (a test
may map to several), an optional note, and optional "conflicts" entries
where observed behavior contradicts an accepted spec (tests themselves are
NOT changed; the orchestrator adjudicates). Docstring statements keep their

def walk(suite):
    for item in suite:
        if isinstance(item, unittest.TestSuite):
            yield from walk(item)
        else:
            yield item


def main():
    suite = unittest.defaultTestLoader.discover(str(OUTPUTS / "tests"))
    entries = []
    unmapped_accepted = []
    conflicts = []
    for test in walk(suite):
        method = getattr(test, test._testMethodName)
        doc = method.__doc__ or ""
        first = doc.strip().splitlines()[0] if doc.strip() else ""
        tag = first.split(" ")[0] if first else ""
        provisional = tag if tag.startswith("S-") else "UNMAPPED"
        prefix = tag.split("-")[1] if tag.startswith("S-") else ""
        entry = {
            "test": test.id(),
            "ear": provisional,
            "earProvisional": provisional,
            "family": FAMILY_BY_PREFIX.get(prefix, "unknown"),
            "kind": "characterization" if "[characterization]" in first else "intended-requirement",
            "statement": first,
            "sources": SOURCE_BY_PREFIX.get(prefix, []),
        }
        if test.id() not in ACCEPTED:
            raise SystemExit("ERROR: no accepted-id reconciliation entry for %s "
                             "(ACCEPTED table in tests/build_mapping.py is out of "
                             "date with the suite)" % test.id())
        rec = ACCEPTED[test.id()]
        for ear in rec["ears"] or []:
            if ear not in ACCEPTED_IDS:
                raise SystemExit("ERROR: %s maps to unknown accepted spec id %r"
                                 % (test.id(), ear))
        ears = rec["ears"]
        entry["ear"] = list(ears) if ears and len(ears) > 1 else (ears[0] if ears else None)
        if rec.get("note"):
            entry["note"] = rec["note"]
        if rec.get("conflicts"):
            entry["conflicts"] = rec["conflicts"]
            for c in rec["conflicts"]:
                conflicts.append({"test": test.id(), "provisional": provisional,
                                  "spec": c["spec"], "reason": c["reason"]})
        if ears is None:
            unmapped_accepted.append(test.id())
        entries.append(entry)
    stale = set(ACCEPTED) - {e["test"] for e in entries}
    if stale:
        raise SystemExit("ERROR: ACCEPTED table has stale entries not in the suite: %s"
                         % sorted(stale))
    missing = [e for e in entries if e["earProvisional"] == "UNMAPPED"]
    mapping = {
        "provisional": False,
        "reconciled": {
            "source": "refs/campaign/orchestrator",
            "commit": "065f18d59875a58a777e212fd12d31d61c693e1e",
            "artifacts": ["rules/*.md", "specs/*.md", "rules/app-inventory.json",
                          "findings/ledger.json"],
            "d004": ("8 gap-closure tests (e03) mapped to the accepted S-IDs named in the "
                     "dispatch: S-BULK-004, S-BULK-005, S-PROJ-008, S-AUTH-010, S-LIFE-009, "
                     "S-QUERY-008, S-DATA-008, S-FORM-010, verified against the spec texts "
                     "at 6f55e3f. At the time the announced integrated tree ce9c7cb had not "
                     "been delivered; the orchestrator has since confirmed the 6f55e3f-based "
                     "reconciliation was correct and accepted it."),
            "d005": ("Full integrated tree 065f18d (analyst batch 2b09f92) merged. The "
                     "3 previously ear:null tests now link to the analyst's new specs: "
                     "test_s_api_014->S-API-008, test_s_act_020->S-LIFE-010, "
                     "test_s_form_024->S-FORM-012. Secondary links added: "
                     "test_s_form_009 (create-trims-title) also ->S-FORM-011, "
                     "test_s_list_024 (titles-unescaped) also ->S-QUERY-009, "
                     "test_s_api_012 (api HTML-passthrough) also ->S-API-007. The "
                     "S-QUERY-003/S-QUERY-005 observables were corrected by the analyst at "
                     "2b09f92 to [3,2,1] and [2,1,3,4] respectively — matching what the "
                     "executed tests assert — and both conflicts now record that resolution "
                     "instead of quoting the superseded enumerations. 137 tests, 0 ear:null."),
            "d006": ("Final sync: the last 2 ear:null orphans link to accepted specs per "
                     "the analyst's inventory v3 adjudication (integrated tree 8d5045e): "
                     "test_s_auth_014 -> S-AUTH-004 (session isolation stated by the "
                     "existing session-creation spec) and test_s_proj_002 -> S-PROJ-001 "
                     "(archive-button render-gating stated by the existing projects-page "
                     "list spec). Mapping-only, no test changes. 137 tests, 0 ear:null."),
            "note": ("'ear' now holds the accepted analyst S-ID(s) (string when one, list "
                     "when several; null when no accepted spec covers the exercised "
                     "behavior — see unmappedAccepted). 'earProvisional' keeps the "
                     "docstring tag for traceability. Docstrings were NOT renamed: the "
                     "provisional and accepted numberings are not aligned "
                     "(S-ACT-NNN -> S-LIFE-* is not mechanical). 'kind' and 'statement' "
                     "are unchanged from the suite docstrings. Where observed behavior "
                     "contradicts an accepted spec the test was NOT changed; the entry "
                     "carries 'conflicts' for orchestrator adjudication."),
        },
        "conflicts": conflicts,
        "unmappedAccepted": unmapped_accepted,
        "note": ("Provisional EARS ids S-<FAMILY>-NNN were derived from the frozen app "
                 "source (commit eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c) and are kept in "
                 "'earProvisional'. kind=characterization marks observed-behavior cases "
                 "labelled separately from intended requirements."),
        "families": FAMILY_BY_PREFIX,
        "testCount": len(entries),
        "unmapped": [e["test"] for e in missing],
        "tests": entries,
    }
    out = OUTPUTS / "reports/tests/mapping.json"
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out.write_text(json.dumps(mapping, indent=2) + "\n")
    print("mapped %d tests (%d unmapped) -> %s" % (len(entries), len(missing), out))
    print("reconciled to accepted analyst ids: %d conflicts, %d without accepted spec"
          % (len(conflicts), len(unmapped_accepted)))
    if missing:
        raise SystemExit(1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
tests/helpers.py

Raw file

"""Shared helpers for the task-tracker test suite.

Real HTTP sessions against http://127.0.0.1:8080 (tt-demo-app) plus database
access through the same podman/psql path the runtime harness uses:
  - fixture()  writes as the app's `tracker` role,
  - sql()      reads as the read-only `oracle_ro` role,
  - reset_db() restores schema.sql + seed.sql (per-test isolation).
"""
import http.client
import json
import subprocess
import unittest
import urllib.parse
from pathlib import Path

BASE_URL = "http://127.0.0.1"
HOST, PORT = "127.0.0.1", 8080
DB_CONTAINER = "tt-demo-db"
APP = Path(__file__).resolve().home() / "app"

# Seed users (see app/src/main/resources/db/seed.sql).
ADMIN = ("admin", "admin")      # role ADMIN   - can approve/reject
MANAGER = ("mgr", "mgr")        # role MANAGER - can approve/reject
MEMBER = ("dev", "dev")         # role MEMBER
VIEWER = ("view", "view")       # role VIEWER


def _psql(user, password, statement, input_text=None):
    cmd = ["podman", "exec", "-e", "PGPASSWORD=" + password, DB_CONTAINER,
           "psql", "-h", "127.0.0.1", "-U", user, "-d", "tracker",
           "-v", "ON_ERROR_STOP=1", "-Atc", statement]
    return subprocess.run(cmd, input=input_text, text=True, capture_output=True)


def fixture(statement):
    """Write to the database as the app role (mirrors `runtime.py fixture`)."""
    result = _psql("tracker", "tracker", statement)
    if result.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError("fixture failed: %s\n%s" % (statement, result.stderr))
    return result.stdout.strip()


def sql(query):
    """Read-only query as oracle_ro (mirrors `runtime.py sql`)."""
    result = _psql("oracle_ro", "oracle_ro", query)
    if result.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError("sql failed: %s\n%s" % (query, result.stderr))
    return result.stdout.strip()


def reset_db():
    """Reload schema.sql + seed.sql exactly like `runtime.py reset`."""
    scripts = [APP / "src/main/resources/db" / name
               for name in ("schema.sql", "seed.sql")]
    script = "\n".join(path.read_text() for path in scripts)
    script += "\nGRANT SELECT ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public TO oracle_ro;\n"
    result = subprocess.run(
        ["podman", "exec", "-i", DB_CONTAINER, "psql", "-U", "tracker",
         "-d", "tracker", "-v", "ON_ERROR_STOP=1"],
        input=script, text=True, capture_output=True)
    if result.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError("reset_db failed:\n%s" % result.stderr)


def tasks_table():
    """Read-only snapshot of the task table keyed by id."""
    raw = sql("SELECT id || '|' || status || '|' || priority || '|' || "
              "coalesce(assignee_id::text, 'NULL') || '|' || coalesce(due_date::text, 'NULL') || '|' || "
              "title || '|' || project_id FROM task ORDER BY id")
    table = {}
    for line in raw.splitlines():
        tid, status, priority, assignee, due, title, project = line.split("|", 6)
        table[int(tid)] = {"status": status, "priority": priority,
                           "assignee_id": None if assignee == "NULL" else int(assignee),
                           "due_date": None if due == "NULL" else due,
                           "title": title, "project_id": int(project)}
    return table


def api_tasks(query=""):
    """GET /api/tasks as JSON (unauthenticated by design)."""
    conn = http.client.HTTPConnection(HOST, PORT, timeout=10)
    conn.request("GET", "/api/tasks" + query)
    body = conn.getresponse().read().decode("utf-8")
    conn.close()
    return json.loads(body)


class Response:
    def __init__(self, status, headers, body):
        self.status = status
        self.headers = dict(headers)
        self.body = body

    @property
    def location(self):
        return self.headers.get("Location")

    def json(self):
        return json.loads(self.body)


class Client:
    """Minimal real-HTTP session; redirects are NOT followed so tests can
    assert status codes and Location headers directly."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.cookie = None

    def request(self, method, path, data=None):
        conn = http.client.HTTPConnection(HOST, PORT, timeout=10)
        headers = {}
        if self.cookie:
            headers["Cookie"] = self.cookie
        body = None
        if data is not None:
            body = urllib.parse.urlencode(data, doseq=True)
            headers["Content-Type"] = "application/x-www-form-urlencoded"
        conn.request(method, path, body=body, headers=headers)
        resp = conn.getresponse()
        content = resp.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")
        set_cookie = resp.getheader("Set-Cookie")
        if set_cookie:
            self.cookie = set_cookie.split(";")[0]
        response = Response(resp.status, resp.getheaders(), content)
        conn.close()
        return response

    def get(self, path, data=None):
        if data:
            path = path + "?" + urllib.parse.urlencode(data, doseq=True)
        return self.request("GET", path)

    def post(self, path, data=None):
        return self.request("POST", path, data=data)

    def login(self, credentials=MEMBER):
        return self.post("/login", {"username": credentials[0],
                                    "password": credentials[1]})


class TrackerTestCase(unittest.TestCase):
    """Base: fresh seeded database per test, separate HTTP session per test."""

    maxDiff = None

    def setUp(self):
        reset_db()
        self.client = Client()
tests/test_api.py

Raw file

"""Batch 4b — public JSON API /api/tasks: readback, filter, escaping, nulls, error boundaries."""
from tests.helpers import TrackerTestCase, fixture, sql


class ApiReadbackTests(TrackerTestCase):

    def test_s_api_001_api_returns_all_seed_tasks_without_authentication(self):
        """S-API-001 — GET /api/tasks without a session answers 200 application/json with all four seed tasks (API is exempt from auth)."""
        r = self.client.get("/api/tasks")
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertTrue(r.headers.get("Content-Type", "").startswith("application/json"))
        rows = r.json()
        self.assertEqual(4, len(rows))

    def test_s_api_002_api_rows_carry_the_documented_field_set(self):
        """S-API-002 — Each API row exposes exactly id, title, status, priority, assigneeId, projectId, dueDate, updatedAt."""
        rows = self.client.get("/api/tasks").json()
        expected = {"id", "title", "status", "priority", "assigneeId", "projectId", "dueDate", "updatedAt"}
        for row in rows:
            self.assertEqual(expected, set(row))

    def test_s_api_003_api_readback_matches_persisted_seed_values(self):
        """S-API-003 — API values match the seeded database state (straight DB readback, not UI rendering)."""
        rows = {row["id"]: row for row in self.client.get("/api/tasks").json()}
        self.assertEqual("Archive old data", rows[4]["title"])
        self.assertEqual("DONE", rows[4]["status"])
        self.assertEqual("LOW", rows[4]["priority"])
        self.assertEqual(1, rows[4]["assigneeId"])
        self.assertEqual(2, rows[4]["projectId"])
        self.assertIsNone(rows[4]["dueDate"])
        self.assertEqual("2026-07-15 18:00:00", rows[4]["updatedAt"])
        self.assertEqual("Design login page", rows[1]["title"])
        self.assertEqual("OPEN", rows[1]["status"])
        self.assertEqual("2026-06-15", rows[1]["dueDate"])

    def test_s_api_004_api_default_order_is_updated_desc(self):
        """S-API-004 — GET /api/tasks lists rows ordered updated_at DESC: [4, 3, 2, 1] on seed data."""
        rows = self.client.get("/api/tasks").json()
        self.assertEqual([4, 3, 2, 1], [row["id"] for row in rows])

    def test_s_api_005_api_reflects_persisted_mutations(self):
        """S-API-005 — The API is a read-back of the DB: a persisted status change and a created task appear on the next read."""
        self.client.login()
        self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 1, "action": "start"})
        self.client.post("/tasks/form", {"title": "Fresh from API", "projectId": 1})
        rows = {row["id"]: row for row in self.client.get("/api/tasks").json()}
        self.assertEqual("IN_PROGRESS", rows[1]["status"])
        created = rows[5]
        self.assertEqual("Fresh from API", created["title"])
        self.assertEqual("OPEN", created["status"])
        self.assertEqual("MEDIUM", created["priority"])


class ApiFilterTests(TrackerTestCase):

    def test_s_api_006_status_filter_returns_only_matching_rows(self):
        """S-API-006 — GET /api/tasks?status=DONE returns only DONE rows, still updated DESC ordered."""
        rows = self.client.get("/api/tasks", {"status": "DONE"}).json()
        self.assertEqual([4], [row["id"] for row in rows])

    def test_s_api_007_status_filter_pending_approval(self):
        """S-API-007 — GET /api/tasks?status=PENDING_APPROVAL returns only [3]."""
        rows = self.client.get("/api/tasks", {"status": "PENDING_APPROVAL"}).json()
        self.assertEqual([3], [row["id"] for row in rows])

    def test_s_api_008_unknown_status_value_returns_empty_array(self):
        """S-API-008 — A status value matching nothing returns a valid empty JSON array, not an error."""
        r = self.client.get("/api/tasks", {"status": "CANCELLED"})
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertEqual([], r.json())

    def test_s_api_009_empty_status_filter_returns_all_rows(self):
        """S-API-009 — An empty status parameter means no filter: all four rows."""
        rows = self.client.get("/api/tasks", {"status": ""}).json()
        self.assertEqual(4, len(rows))


class ApiEscapingAndNullTests(TrackerTestCase):

    def test_s_api_010_null_assignee_and_due_date_serialize_as_json_null(self):
        """S-API-010 — A task with no assignee and no due date serializes assigneeId and dueDate as JSON null."""
        fixture("INSERT INTO task (title, description, status, priority, assignee_id, project_id, due_date) "
                "VALUES ('Orphan', NULL, 'OPEN', 'LOW', NULL, 1, NULL)")
        rows = self.client.get("/api/tasks").json()
        orphan = next(row for row in rows if row["title"] == "Orphan")
        self.assertIsNone(orphan["assigneeId"])
        self.assertIsNone(orphan["dueDate"])

    def test_s_api_011_quotes_and_backslashes_are_escaped_to_valid_json(self):
        """S-API-011 — A title containing double quotes and backslashes is escaped in the JSON output; the payload parses and round-trips exactly."""
        title = 'Quo"te\\Path'
        fixture("INSERT INTO task (title, description, status, priority, assignee_id, project_id, due_date) "
                "VALUES ('" + title + "', NULL, 'OPEN', 'LOW', NULL, 1, NULL)")
        rows = self.client.get("/api/tasks").json()
        self.assertIn(title, [row["title"] for row in rows])

    def test_s_api_012_html_special_characters_pass_through_unescaped(self):
        """S-API-012 [characterization] — The hand-rolled esc() only escapes backslash and quote; HTML-significant characters (<, >, &) pass through raw. The payload is still valid JSON."""
        fixture("INSERT INTO task (title, description, status, priority, assignee_id, project_id, due_date) "
                "VALUES ('<script>alert(1)</script>&', NULL, 'OPEN', 'LOW', NULL, 1, NULL)")
        rows = self.client.get("/api/tasks").json()
        self.assertIn("<script>alert(1)</script>&", [row["title"] for row in rows])

    def test_s_api_013_api_body_is_always_valid_json_even_with_hostile_titles(self):
        """S-API-013 — The response body parses as JSON for a table mixing escaped, null-bearing and plain rows."""
        title = 'Mi\\xed"content'
        fixture("INSERT INTO task (title, description, status, priority, assignee_id, project_id, due_date) "
                "VALUES ('" + title + "', NULL, 'OPEN', 'HIGH', 2, 1, '2026-08-08')")
        rows = self.client.get("/api/tasks").json()
        self.assertEqual(5, len(rows))
        mixed = next(row for row in rows if row["id"] == 5)
        self.assertEqual(title, mixed["title"])
        self.assertEqual(2, mixed["assigneeId"])
        self.assertEqual("2026-08-08", mixed["dueDate"])

    def test_s_api_014_api_ignores_post_method(self):
        """S-API-014 — The API servlet implements GET only; POST /api/tasks is rejected with 405 and mutates nothing."""
        r = self.client.post("/api/tasks", {"title": "nope"})
        self.assertEqual(405, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("4", sql("SELECT count(*) FROM task"))

"""Batch 1a — authentication, session, logout, access exemptions, approve roles."""
from tests.helpers import ADMIN, MANAGER, MEMBER, VIEWER, TrackerTestCase, sql, tasks_table


class UnauthenticatedAccessTests(TrackerTestCase):
    """Every protected route refuses anonymous access with a redirect and no mutation."""

    def test_s_auth_001_unauth_get_tasks_redirects_to_login(self):
        """S-AUTH-001 — Unauthenticated GET /tasks is redirected to /login."""
        r = self.client.get("/tasks")
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertTrue(r.location.endswith("/login"), r.location)

    def test_s_auth_002_unauth_get_task_form_redirects_to_login(self):
        """S-AUTH-002 — Unauthenticated GET /tasks/form is redirected to /login."""
        r = self.client.get("/tasks/form", {"id": 1})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertTrue(r.location.endswith("/login"), r.location)

    def test_s_auth_003_unauth_post_task_action_redirects_without_mutation(self):
        """S-AUTH-003 — Unauthenticated POST /tasks/action is redirected to /login and mutates nothing."""
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 1, "action": "start"})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertTrue(r.location.endswith("/login"), r.location)
        self.assertEqual("OPEN", tasks_table()[1]["status"])

    def test_s_auth_004_unauth_get_projects_redirects_to_login(self):
        """S-AUTH-004 — Unauthenticated GET /projects is redirected to /login."""
        r = self.client.get("/projects")
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertTrue(r.location.endswith("/login"), r.location)

    def test_s_auth_005_unauth_post_projects_redirects_without_mutation(self):
        """S-AUTH-005 — Unauthenticated POST /projects is redirected to /login and creates no project."""
        r = self.client.post("/projects", {"name": "Sneaky", "description": "x"})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertTrue(r.location.endswith("/login"), r.location)
        self.assertEqual("3", sql("SELECT count(*) FROM project"))

    def test_s_auth_006_unauth_logout_redirects_to_login(self):
        """S-AUTH-006 — Unauthenticated GET /logout is redirected to /login (logout itself is protected)."""
        r = self.client.get("/logout")
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertTrue(r.location.endswith("/login"), r.location)

    def test_s_auth_007_api_tasks_is_exempt_from_authentication(self):
        """S-AUTH-007 — GET /api/tasks answers 200 without a session (API is exempted by AuthFilter)."""
        r = self.client.get("/api/tasks")
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertEqual(4, len(r.json()))

    def test_s_auth_008_styles_css_is_exempt_from_authentication(self):
        """S-AUTH-008 — GET /styles.css answers 200 without a session (static CSS is exempted)."""
        r = self.client.get("/styles.css")
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)

    def test_s_auth_009_index_redirects_to_login_for_anonymous(self):
        """S-AUTH-009 — Unauthenticated GET / (welcome page) is redirected to /login like other protected paths."""
        r = self.client.get("/")
        self.assertIn(r.status, (302, 200))
        if r.status == 302:
            self.assertTrue(r.location.endswith("/login"), r.location)


class LoginTests(TrackerTestCase):
    """POST /login credential handling and session establishment."""

    def test_s_auth_010_login_page_renders_sign_in_form(self):
        """S-AUTH-010 — GET /login renders the sign-in form with username and password fields."""
        r = self.client.get("/login")
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertIn("<h1>Sign in</h1>", r.body)
        self.assertIn('name="username"', r.body)
        self.assertIn('name="password"', r.body)

    def test_s_auth_011_login_with_valid_member_credentials_redirects_to_tasks(self):
        """S-AUTH-011 — POST /login with valid credentials redirects to /tasks and establishes a session."""
        r = self.client.login(MEMBER)
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertTrue(r.location.endswith("/tasks"), r.location)
        self.assertIsNotNone(self.client.cookie, "expected a session cookie after login")
        page = self.client.get("/tasks")
        self.assertEqual(200, page.status)

    def test_s_auth_012_login_shows_error_for_wrong_password(self):
        """S-AUTH-012 — POST /login with a wrong password re-renders the form with an error and grants no access (a container session id may be issued, but it authenticates nothing)."""
        r = self.client.post("/login", {"username": "dev", "password": "wrong"})
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertIn("Invalid credentials", r.body)
        self.assertEqual(302, self.client.get("/tasks").status,
                         "the post-failure session must not be authenticated")

    def test_s_auth_013_login_shows_error_for_unknown_username(self):
        """S-AUTH-013 — POST /login with an unknown username shows the same generic error and grants no access."""
        r = self.client.post("/login", {"username": "nobody", "password": "dev"})
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertIn("Invalid credentials", r.body)
        self.assertEqual(302, self.client.get("/tasks").status)

    def test_s_auth_014_login_session_is_scoped_to_one_client(self):
        """S-AUTH-014 — A session created by one client does not authenticate a different client."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        other = type(self.client)()
        self.assertEqual(302, other.get("/tasks").status)

    def test_s_auth_015_login_as_each_seeded_role_succeeds(self):
        """S-AUTH-015 — Each seeded account (admin/mgr/dev/view) can authenticate."""
        for creds in (ADMIN, MANAGER, MEMBER, VIEWER):
            client = type(self.client)()
            r = client.login(creds)
            self.assertEqual(302, r.status, creds)
            self.assertTrue(r.location.endswith("/tasks"), creds)

    def test_s_auth_023_authenticated_header_shows_full_name_role_and_logout(self):
        """S-AUTH-010 — After login as mgr, GET /tasks renders the shared header with the user's full name and role ('Morgan Manager (MANAGER)') and a logout link (render-only)."""
        self.client.login(MANAGER)
        r = self.client.get("/tasks")
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertIn("Morgan Manager (MANAGER)", r.body)
        self.assertIn('href="/logout"', r.body)


class LogoutTests(TrackerTestCase):

    def test_s_auth_016_logout_invalidates_session_and_redirects_to_login(self):
        """S-AUTH-016 — GET /logout invalidates the session and redirects to /login; the old cookie no longer authenticates."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.get("/logout")
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertTrue(r.location.endswith("/login"), r.location)
        self.assertEqual(302, self.client.get("/tasks").status)

    def test_s_auth_017_logout_without_session_redirects_to_login(self):
        """S-AUTH-017 — GET /logout with no session still redirects to /login without an error."""
        r = self.client.get("/logout")
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertTrue(r.location.endswith("/login"), r.location)


class ApproveRoleTests(TrackerTestCase):
    """Approve/reject is restricted to ADMIN and MANAGER (filter-level gate)."""

    def test_s_auth_018_member_cannot_approve_gets_403_without_mutation(self):
        """S-AUTH-018 — MEMBER posting action=approve gets 403 and the task stays PENDING_APPROVAL."""
