        self.assertEqual(403, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("PENDING_APPROVAL", tasks_table()[3]["status"])

    def test_s_auth_019_viewer_cannot_approve_gets_403_without_mutation(self):
        """S-AUTH-019 — VIEWER posting action=approve gets 403 and the task stays PENDING_APPROVAL."""
        self.client.login(VIEWER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 3, "action": "approve"})
        self.assertEqual(403, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("PENDING_APPROVAL", tasks_table()[3]["status"])

    def test_s_auth_020_member_cannot_reject_gets_403_without_mutation(self):
        """S-AUTH-020 — MEMBER posting action=reject gets 403 and the task stays PENDING_APPROVAL."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 3, "action": "reject"})
        self.assertEqual(403, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("PENDING_APPROVAL", tasks_table()[3]["status"])

    def test_s_auth_021_manager_can_approve_persistent_effect(self):
        """S-AUTH-021 — MANAGER posting action=approve is permitted and persists DONE."""
        self.client.login(MANAGER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 3, "action": "approve"})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("DONE", tasks_table()[3]["status"])

    def test_s_auth_022_admin_can_reject_persistent_effect(self):
        """S-AUTH-022 — ADMIN posting action=reject is permitted and persists IN_PROGRESS."""
        self.client.login(ADMIN)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 3, "action": "reject"})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("IN_PROGRESS", tasks_table()[3]["status"])
tests/test_bulk_close.py

Raw file

"""Batch 3b — bulk close: empty, selected, and mixed states; d004 gap closure."""
import re

from tests.helpers import TrackerTestCase, sql, tasks_table


def updated_at(task_id):
    return sql("SELECT to_char(updated_at, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS') FROM task WHERE id = %d" % task_id)


class BulkCloseTests(TrackerTestCase):

    def test_s_bulk_001_bulk_close_without_ids_redirects_and_mutates_nothing(self):
        """S-BULK-001 — POSTing bulkClose with no ids redirects to /tasks and leaves every task untouched."""
        self.client.login()
        before = tasks_table()
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"action": "bulkClose"})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertTrue(r.location.endswith("/tasks"), r.location)
        self.assertEqual(before, tasks_table())

    def test_s_bulk_002_bulk_close_single_selected_open_task(self):
        """S-BULK-002 — bulkClose with one selected OPEN task persists exactly that task as DONE."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"action": "bulkClose", "ids": "1"})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        table = tasks_table()
        self.assertEqual("DONE", table[1]["status"])
        self.assertEqual("IN_PROGRESS", table[2]["status"])
        self.assertEqual("PENDING_APPROVAL", table[3]["status"])

    def test_s_bulk_003_bulk_close_all_selected_tasks(self):
        """S-BULK-003 — bulkClose with all four tasks selected closes every non-DONE task (statuses OPEN, IN_PROGRESS, PENDING_APPROVAL all become DONE)."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action",
                             {"action": "bulkClose", "ids": ["1", "2", "3", "4"]})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        table = tasks_table()
        self.assertEqual({"DONE"}, {t["status"] for t in table.values()})

    def test_s_bulk_004_bulk_close_skips_already_done_tasks(self):
        """S-BULK-004 — bulkClose only writes status<>'DONE': a mixed selection leaves an already-DONE task untouched (status and updated_at unchanged)."""
        self.client.login()
        before_done_updated = updated_at(4)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"action": "bulkClose", "ids": ["1", "4"]})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        table = tasks_table()
        self.assertEqual("DONE", table[1]["status"])
        self.assertEqual("DONE", table[4]["status"])
        self.assertEqual(before_done_updated, updated_at(4),
                         "bulk close must not touch an already-DONE row's updated_at")

    def test_s_bulk_005_bulk_close_mixed_states_closes_only_non_done(self):
        """S-BULK-005 — bulkClose over a mixed-state selection (OPEN + IN_PROGRESS + PENDING_APPROVAL + DONE) persists DONE for the three non-DONE rows."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action",
                             {"action": "bulkClose", "ids": ["1", "2", "3", "4"]})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        table = tasks_table()
        self.assertEqual("DONE", table[1]["status"])
        self.assertEqual("DONE", table[2]["status"])
        self.assertEqual("DONE", table[3]["status"])
        self.assertEqual("DONE", table[4]["status"])

    def test_s_bulk_006_bulk_close_with_unknown_ids_ignores_them(self):
        """S-BULK-006 — bulkClose with nonexistent ids mixed into a real selection closes the real tasks and ignores the unknown ids without error."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action",
                             {"action": "bulkClose", "ids": ["1", "999"]})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("DONE", tasks_table()[1]["status"])
        self.assertEqual("IN_PROGRESS", tasks_table()[2]["status"])

    def test_s_bulk_007_bulk_close_only_unknown_ids_is_a_safe_noop(self):
        """S-BULK-007 — bulkClose with only nonexistent ids redirects without error and mutates nothing."""
        self.client.login()
        before = tasks_table()
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"action": "bulkClose", "ids": "999"})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertEqual(before, tasks_table())

    def test_s_bulk_008_bulk_close_with_malformed_id_yields_500(self):
        """S-BULK-008 [characterization] — bulkClose with a non-numeric id (abc) surfaces a 500 (uncaught NumberFormatException)."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"action": "bulkClose", "ids": "abc"})
        self.assertEqual(500, r.status)

    def test_s_bulk_009_bulk_close_requires_authentication(self):
        """S-BULK-009 — bulkClose without a session is redirected to /login and closes nothing."""
        before = tasks_table()
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"action": "bulkClose", "ids": ["1", "2", "3"]})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertTrue(r.location.endswith("/login"), r.location)
        self.assertEqual(before, tasks_table())

    def test_s_bulk_010_member_bulk_close_bypasses_approval_on_pending_task(self):
        """S-BULK-004 [characterization] — A MEMBER (dev) posting bulkClose&ids=3 closes the PENDING_APPROVAL task with 302 and no 403 anywhere (observed approval bypass, defect D-002); the approve action it replaces is 403 for the same user on the same task."""
        self.client.login()  # dev / MEMBER
        r_approve = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"action": "approve", "id": 3})
        self.assertEqual(403, r_approve.status)
        self.assertEqual("PENDING_APPROVAL", tasks_table()[3]["status"],
                         "the 403 must have blocked the approve mutation")
        r_bulk = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"action": "bulkClose", "ids": 3})
        self.assertEqual(302, r_bulk.status)
        self.assertFalse(r_bulk.location.endswith("/login"))
        self.assertEqual("DONE", tasks_table()[3]["status"],
                         "observed: bulkClose writes DONE over PENDING_APPROVAL without any role check")

    def test_s_bulk_011_bulk_close_form_renders_checkbox_per_row_and_submit_button(self):
        """S-BULK-005 — GET /tasks renders one bulkClose form: hidden action=bulkClose, one ids checkbox per task row (1..4), and a 'Close selected (bulk)' submit button (render-only)."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks")
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertIn('name="action" value="bulkClose"', r.body)
        checkboxes = re.findall(r'<input type="checkbox" name="ids" value="(\d+)"/>', r.body)
        self.assertEqual(["1", "2", "3", "4"], sorted(checkboxes),
                         "one ids checkbox per task row, whatever the list order")
        self.assertIn("Close selected (bulk)", r.body)
tests/test_projects.py

Raw file

"""Batch 1b — projects list/create/archive/validation; d004 archived-project task boundary."""
import re

from tests.helpers import MEMBER, TrackerTestCase, sql, tasks_table


def projects_table():
    raw = sql("SELECT id || '|' || name || '|' || state || '|' || owner_id || '|' || "
              "coalesce(description, 'NULL') FROM project ORDER BY id")
    table = {}
    for line in raw.splitlines():
        pid, name, state, owner, description = line.split("|", 4)
        table[int(pid)] = {"name": name, "state": state, "owner_id": int(owner),
                           "description": None if description == "NULL" else description}
    return table


class ProjectListTests(TrackerTestCase):

    def test_s_proj_001_projects_page_lists_seeded_projects_ordered_by_name(self):
        """S-PROJ-001 — GET /projects lists all seeded projects in name order with state and owner."""
        r = self.client.login(MEMBER)
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        page = self.client.get("/projects")
        self.assertEqual(200, page.status)
        self.assertIn("Apollo", page.body)
        self.assertIn("Borealis", page.body)
        self.assertIn("Legacy Archive", page.body)
        i_apollo = page.body.index("Apollo")
        i_borealis = page.body.index("Borealis")
        i_legacy = page.body.index("Legacy Archive")
        self.assertLess(i_apollo, i_borealis)
        self.assertLess(i_borealis, i_legacy)
        self.assertIn("ARCHIVED", page.body)

    def test_s_proj_002_projects_page_marks_archived_project_without_archive_button(self):
        """S-PROJ-002 — The projects page shows the Archive action only for ACTIVE projects."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        page = self.client.get("/projects")
        rows = page.body.split("<tr>")
        archived_row = next(row for row in rows if "Legacy Archive" in row)
        active_row = next(row for row in rows if "Apollo" in row)
        self.assertNotIn('value="archive"', archived_row)
        self.assertIn('value="archive"', active_row)


class ProjectCreateTests(TrackerTestCase):

    def test_s_proj_003_create_project_persists_active_project_owned_by_current_user(self):
        """S-PROJ-003 — POST /projects with a name creates an ACTIVE project owned by the signed-in user and redirects to /projects."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/projects", {"name": "Zenith", "description": "New work"})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertTrue(r.location.endswith("/projects"), r.location)
        created = [p for p in projects_table().values() if p["name"] == "Zenith"]
        self.assertEqual(1, len(created))
        self.assertEqual("ACTIVE", created[0]["state"])
        self.assertEqual("New work", created[0]["description"])
        self.assertEqual(3, created[0]["owner_id"], "owner must be the signed-in user (dev, id 3)")

    def test_s_proj_004_create_project_trims_surrounding_whitespace_from_name(self):
        """S-PROJ-004 — A project name with surrounding whitespace is trimmed before persisting."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/projects", {"name": "  Padded  "})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        names = [p["name"] for p in projects_table().values()]
        self.assertIn("Padded", names)
        self.assertNotIn("  Padded  ", names)

    def test_s_proj_005_create_project_without_name_is_refused_with_400(self):
        """S-PROJ-005 — POST /projects without a name is refused with 400 and persists nothing."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/projects", {"name": "", "description": "x"})
        self.assertEqual(400, r.status)
        self.assertIn("Project name is required", r.body)
        self.assertEqual("3", sql("SELECT count(*) FROM project"))

    def test_s_proj_006_create_project_with_whitespace_only_name_is_refused_with_400(self):
        """S-PROJ-006 — POST /projects with a whitespace-only name is refused with 400 and persists nothing."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/projects", {"name": "   "})
        self.assertEqual(400, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("3", sql("SELECT count(*) FROM project"))

    def test_s_proj_007_create_project_with_omitted_name_field_is_refused_with_400(self):
        """S-PROJ-007 — POST /projects with no name field at all (name=null) is refused with 400 and persists nothing."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/projects", {"description": "x"})
        self.assertEqual(400, r.status)
        self.assertIn("Project name is required", r.body)
        self.assertEqual("3", sql("SELECT count(*) FROM project"))

    def test_s_proj_008_create_project_without_description_persists_null_description(self):
        """S-PROJ-008 — Creating a project with no description persists a NULL description."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/projects", {"name": "NoDesc"})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        created = [p for p in projects_table().values() if p["name"] == "NoDesc"]
        self.assertEqual(1, len(created))
        self.assertIsNone(created[0]["description"])

    def test_s_proj_009_created_project_appears_in_name_ordered_list(self):
        """S-PROJ-009 — A newly created project is listed in correct name order on /projects."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        self.client.post("/projects", {"name": "Aardvark"})
        page = self.client.get("/projects")
        i_aardvark = page.body.index("Aardvark")
        i_apollo = page.body.index("Apollo")
        self.assertLess(i_aardvark, i_apollo, "Aardvark must sort before Apollo")


class ProjectArchiveTests(TrackerTestCase):

    def test_s_proj_010_archive_active_project_persists_archived_state(self):
        """S-PROJ-009 — POST /projects action=archive on an ACTIVE project persists state ARCHIVED and redirects."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/projects", {"action": "archive", "id": 1})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertTrue(r.location.endswith("/projects"), r.location)
        self.assertEqual("ARCHIVED", projects_table()[1]["state"])

    def test_s_proj_011_archive_is_idempotent_guarded_to_active_state(self):
        """S-PROJ-010 — Archiving an already-ARCHIVED project is a guarded no-op (state stays ARCHIVED, no error)."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/projects", {"action": "archive", "id": 3})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("ARCHIVED", projects_table()[3]["state"])

    def test_s_proj_012_archive_unknown_project_id_redirects_without_error(self):
        """S-PROJ-011 — Archiving a nonexistent project id redirects with no visible error and mutates nothing."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/projects", {"action": "archive", "id": 999})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        states = sorted({p["state"] for p in projects_table().values()})
        self.assertEqual(["ACTIVE", "ARCHIVED"], states)

    def test_s_proj_013_archive_with_malformed_id_yields_500_error_page(self):
        """S-PROJ-012 [characterization] — Archiving with a non-numeric id (abc) surfaces a 500 server error (uncaught NumberFormatException)."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/projects", {"action": "archive", "id": "abc"})
        self.assertEqual(500, r.status)

    def test_s_proj_014_projects_delete_link_targets_an_unmapped_route(self):
        """S-PROJ-013 [characterization] — The projects page renders a Delete link to /projects/delete, but no servlet is mapped there; following it yields 404. Defect candidate: dangling destructive affordance."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        page = self.client.get("/projects")
        self.assertIn("/projects/delete?id=1", page.body)
        r = self.client.get("/projects/delete", {"id": 1})
        self.assertEqual(404, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("3", sql("SELECT count(*) FROM project"),
                         "the dangling delete link must not have removed anything")


class ProjectTaskStateTests(TrackerTestCase):
    """d004 — task/project state boundary: archived projects still accept tasks."""

    def test_s_proj_015_task_create_and_edit_in_archived_project_are_accepted(self):
        """S-PROJ-008 [characterization] — POST /tasks/form with projectId=3 (ARCHIVED 'Legacy Archive') succeeds with 302 ...saved=1 and persists, and the new task can then be edited in the same archived project (observed: no project-state check exists)."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {"title": "Ghost task", "projectId": 3})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertRegex(r.location, r"/tasks/form\?id=\d+&saved=1$")
        new_id = int(re.search(r"id=(\d+)", r.location).group(1))
        row = sql("SELECT t.title || '|' || p.name || '|' || p.state FROM task t "
                  "JOIN project p ON p.id = t.project_id WHERE t.id = %d" % new_id)
        self.assertEqual("Ghost task|Legacy Archive|ARCHIVED", row)
        r_edit = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {"id": new_id, "title": "Ghost task edited",
                                                  "projectId": 3})
        self.assertEqual(302, r_edit.status)
        self.assertEqual("Ghost task edited", tasks_table()[new_id]["title"])
tests/test_task_actions.py

Raw file

"""Batch 3a — task start/submit/approve/reject/reopen, state guards, not-found ids, unknown actions; d004 render check."""
import re

from tests.helpers import MANAGER, TrackerTestCase, tasks_table


def row_actions(body, task_id):
    """The transition-button action values rendered inside a list row (by its ids checkbox)."""
    for row in re.findall(r"<tr>.*?</tr>", body, re.S):
        if 'name="ids" value="%d"' % task_id in row:
            return re.findall(r'name="action" value="([a-z_]+)"', row)
    return None


class TaskLifecycleTests(TrackerTestCase):
    """Happy-path transitions OPEN → IN_PROGRESS → PENDING_APPROVAL → DONE → OPEN."""

    def test_s_act_001_start_moves_open_task_to_in_progress_and_redirects_to_list(self):
        """S-ACT-001 — action=start on an OPEN task persists IN_PROGRESS and redirects to /tasks."""
        r = self.client.login()
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 1, "action": "start"})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertTrue(r.location.endswith("/tasks"), r.location)
        self.assertEqual("IN_PROGRESS", tasks_table()[1]["status"])

    def test_s_act_002_submit_moves_in_progress_task_to_pending_approval(self):
        """S-ACT-002 — action=submit on an IN_PROGRESS task persists PENDING_APPROVAL."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 2, "action": "submit"})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("PENDING_APPROVAL", tasks_table()[2]["status"])

    def test_s_act_003_approve_moves_pending_task_to_done(self):
        """S-ACT-003 — action=approve by an approver on a PENDING_APPROVAL task persists DONE."""
        self.client.login(MANAGER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 3, "action": "approve"})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("DONE", tasks_table()[3]["status"])

    def test_s_act_004_reject_moves_pending_task_back_to_in_progress(self):
        """S-ACT-004 — action=reject by an approver on a PENDING_APPROVAL task persists IN_PROGRESS."""
        self.client.login(MANAGER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 3, "action": "reject"})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("IN_PROGRESS", tasks_table()[3]["status"])

    def test_s_act_005_reopen_moves_done_task_back_to_open(self):
        """S-ACT-005 — action=reopen on a DONE task persists OPEN."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 4, "action": "reopen"})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("OPEN", tasks_table()[4]["status"])

    def test_s_act_006_full_lifecycle_walk_persists_each_step(self):
        """S-ACT-006 — A task can walk the whole lifecycle: start, submit, approve, reopen, then start again."""
        self.client.login(MANAGER)
        actions = [("start", "IN_PROGRESS"), ("submit", "PENDING_APPROVAL"),
                   ("approve", "DONE"), ("reopen", "OPEN"), ("start", "IN_PROGRESS")]
        for action, expected in actions:
            r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 1, "action": action})
            self.assertEqual(302, r.status, action)
            self.assertEqual(expected, tasks_table()[1]["status"], action)

    def test_s_act_007_action_bumps_updated_at(self):
        """S-ACT-007 — A successful transition refreshes the task's updated_at timestamp."""
        before = tasks_table()[1]
        self.client.login()
        self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 1, "action": "start"})
        after = tasks_table()[1]
        self.assertNotEqual(before, after)
        self.assertEqual("IN_PROGRESS", after["status"])

    def test_s_act_008_return_detail_redirects_to_task_detail_page(self):
        """S-ACT-008 — A successful action with return=detail redirects to /tasks/form?id=<id> instead of the list."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 1, "action": "start", "return": "detail"})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertTrue(r.location.endswith("/tasks/form?id=1"), r.location)

    def test_s_act_021_list_shows_exactly_the_valid_transition_buttons_per_status(self):
        """S-LIFE-009 — GET /tasks renders per row exactly the buttons valid for its current status: OPEN→[start], IN_PROGRESS→[submit], PENDING_APPROVAL→[approve, reject], DONE→[reopen] (render-only, no mutation)."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks")
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertEqual(["start"], row_actions(r.body, 1))
        self.assertEqual(["submit"], row_actions(r.body, 2))
        self.assertEqual(["approve", "reject"], row_actions(r.body, 3))
        self.assertEqual(["reopen"], row_actions(r.body, 4))


class StateGuardTests(TrackerTestCase):
    """Every action checks the current status before writing; wrong state is a 409."""

    def test_s_act_009_start_refused_when_not_open_409_without_mutation(self):
        """S-ACT-009 — action=start on an IN_PROGRESS task returns 409 with the guard message and mutates nothing."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 2, "action": "start"})
        self.assertEqual(409, r.status)
        self.assertIn("Cannot start a task in status IN_PROGRESS (expected OPEN)", r.body)
        self.assertEqual("IN_PROGRESS", tasks_table()[2]["status"])

    def test_s_act_010_submit_refused_when_not_in_progress_409(self):
        """S-ACT-010 — action=submit on an OPEN task returns 409 and mutates nothing."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 1, "action": "submit"})
        self.assertEqual(409, r.status)
        self.assertIn("expected IN_PROGRESS", r.body)
        self.assertEqual("OPEN", tasks_table()[1]["status"])

    def test_s_act_011_approve_refused_when_not_pending_409(self):
        """S-ACT-011 — action=approve on an OPEN task returns 409 and mutates nothing."""
        self.client.login(MANAGER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 1, "action": "approve"})
        self.assertEqual(409, r.status)
        self.assertIn("expected PENDING_APPROVAL", r.body)
        self.assertEqual("OPEN", tasks_table()[1]["status"])

    def test_s_act_012_reject_refused_when_done_409(self):
        """S-ACT-012 — action=reject on a DONE task returns 409 and mutates nothing."""
        self.client.login(MANAGER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 4, "action": "reject"})
        self.assertEqual(409, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("DONE", tasks_table()[4]["status"])

    def test_s_act_013_reopen_refused_when_not_done_409(self):
        """S-ACT-013 — action=reopen on an OPEN task returns 409 and mutates nothing."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 1, "action": "reopen"})
        self.assertEqual(409, r.status)
        self.assertIn("expected DONE", r.body)
        self.assertEqual("OPEN", tasks_table()[1]["status"])

    def test_s_act_014_all_transitions_refused_on_done_task_except_reopen(self):
        """S-ACT-014 — A DONE task refuses start/submit with 409; only reopen applies (approve/reject need PENDING_APPROVAL)."""
        self.client.login(MANAGER)
        for action in ("start", "submit", "approve", "reject"):
            r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 4, "action": action})
            self.assertEqual(409, r.status, action)
            self.assertEqual("DONE", tasks_table()[4]["status"], action)


class ActionErrorBoundaryTests(TrackerTestCase):

    def test_s_act_015_action_on_nonexistent_task_returns_404_without_mutation(self):
        """S-ACT-015 — Any action for a nonexistent task id returns 404 "No such task" and mutates nothing."""
        self.client.login()
        for action in ("start", "submit", "reopen"):
            r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 999, "action": action})
            self.assertEqual(404, r.status, action)
            self.assertIn("No such task", r.body)
        self.assertEqual(4, len(tasks_table()))

    def test_s_act_016_unknown_action_returns_400_without_mutation(self):
        """S-ACT-016 — An unknown action returns 400 "Unknown action" and mutates nothing."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 1, "action": "frobnicate"})
        self.assertEqual(400, r.status)
        self.assertIn("Unknown action", r.body)
        self.assertEqual("OPEN", tasks_table()[1]["status"])

    def test_s_act_017_missing_action_returns_400(self):
        """S-ACT-017 [characterization] — POSTing /tasks/action with no action parameter returns 400 "Unknown action: null"."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 1})
        self.assertEqual(400, r.status)
        self.assertIn("Unknown action: null", r.body)

    def test_s_act_018_malformed_task_id_yields_500(self):
        """S-ACT-018 [characterization] — A non-numeric task id (abc) surfaces a 500 (uncaught NumberFormatException before the 404 check)."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": "abc", "action": "start"})
        self.assertEqual(500, r.status)

    def test_s_act_019_missing_task_id_yields_500(self):
        """S-ACT-019 [characterization] — POSTing an action with no id parameter surfaces a 500 (NumberFormatException on null)."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"action": "start"})
        self.assertEqual(500, r.status)

    def test_s_act_020_action_rejects_get_method(self):
        """S-ACT-020 — GET /tasks/action is not supported (the servlet only implements POST)."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks/action", {"id": 1, "action": "start"})
        self.assertEqual(405, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("OPEN", tasks_table()[1]["status"])

"""Batch 2 — task form display, create, edit, validation, defaults, nullable fields; d004 column-width boundary."""
import re

from tests.helpers import MEMBER, TrackerTestCase, fixture, sql, tasks_table


class TaskFormDisplayTests(TrackerTestCase):

    def test_s_form_001_blank_form_renders_new_task_with_defaults(self):
        """S-FORM-001 — GET /tasks/form without id renders a blank "New task" form with all people and projects offered."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.get("/tasks/form")
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertIn("<h1>New task</h1>", r.body)
        self.assertIn('name="title"', r.body)
        self.assertIn('name="description"', r.body)
        self.assertIn('name="projectId"', r.body)
        self.assertIn('name="assigneeId"', r.body)
        self.assertIn('name="priority"', r.body)
        self.assertIn('name="dueDate"', r.body)
        for person in ("Ada Admin", "Morgan Manager", "Devon Member", "Vic Viewer"):
            self.assertIn(person, r.body)
        for project in ("Apollo", "Borealis", "Legacy Archive"):
            self.assertIn(project, r.body)

    def test_s_form_002_form_with_id_prefills_existing_task(self):
        """S-FORM-002 — GET /tasks/form?id=1 renders the task detail pre-filled with the persisted values."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.get("/tasks/form", {"id": 1})
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertIn("<h1>Task #1: Design login page</h1>", r.body)
        self.assertIn('value="Design login page"', r.body)
        self.assertIn("Mockups + a11y pass", r.body)
        self.assertIn('value="2026-06-15"', r.body)
        self.assertIn('selected', r.body)

    def test_s_form_003_form_with_unknown_id_renders_blank_new_task_form(self):
        """S-FORM-003 [characterization] — GET /tasks/form?id=999 silently renders a blank "New task" form instead of 404."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.get("/tasks/form", {"id": 999})
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertIn("<h1>New task</h1>", r.body)
        self.assertNotIn("Task #", r.body.split("</h1>")[0])

    def test_s_form_004_form_with_malformed_id_yields_500(self):
        """S-FORM-004 [characterization] — GET /tasks/form?id=abc surfaces a 500 server error (uncaught NumberFormatException)."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.get("/tasks/form", {"id": "abc"})
        self.assertEqual(500, r.status)

    def test_s_form_005_saved_banner_shown_after_redirect_with_saved_flag(self):
        """S-FORM-005 — The form page shows a Saved banner when redirected with saved=1 after a successful save."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        self.client.post("/tasks/form", {"title": "Banner check", "projectId": 1})
        r = self.client.get("/tasks/form", {"id": 5, "saved": 1})
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertIn("Saved.", r.body)

    def test_s_form_006_empty_id_parameter_renders_blank_form(self):
        """S-FORM-006 [characterization] — GET /tasks/form?id= (present but empty) is treated as "no id": a blank New task form renders."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.get("/tasks/form", {"id": ""})
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertIn("<h1>New task</h1>", r.body)


class TaskCreateTests(TrackerTestCase):

    def test_s_form_007_create_minimal_task_defaults_status_priority_and_nullables(self):
        """S-FORM-007 — POST /tasks/form with only title+projectId persists status OPEN, priority MEDIUM, and NULL assignee/due date, then redirects to the saved task's detail."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {"title": "Bare task", "projectId": 1})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertIn("/tasks/form?id=5", r.location)
        self.assertIn("saved=1", r.location)
        row = tasks_table()[5]
        self.assertEqual("OPEN", row["status"])
        self.assertEqual("MEDIUM", row["priority"])
        self.assertIsNone(row["assignee_id"])
        self.assertIsNone(row["due_date"])
        self.assertEqual(1, row["project_id"])

    def test_s_form_008_create_full_task_persists_every_field(self):
        """S-FORM-008 — POST /tasks/form with every field persists title, description, priority, assignee, project and due date."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {
            "title": "Full task", "description": "All fields",
            "priority": "HIGH", "projectId": 2, "assigneeId": 3,
            "dueDate": "2026-12-01"})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        row = tasks_table()[5]
        self.assertEqual("Full task", row["title"])
        self.assertEqual("All fields", sql("SELECT description FROM task WHERE id = 5"))
        self.assertEqual("HIGH", row["priority"])
        self.assertEqual(3, row["assignee_id"])
        self.assertEqual(2, row["project_id"])
        self.assertEqual("2026-12-01", row["due_date"])

    def test_s_form_009_create_trims_surrounding_whitespace_from_title(self):
        """S-FORM-009 — A task title with surrounding whitespace is trimmed before persisting."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {"title": "  Trimmed task  ", "projectId": 1})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("Trimmed task", tasks_table()[5]["title"])

    def test_s_form_010_create_with_blank_title_is_refused_without_mutation(self):
        """S-FORM-010 — POST /tasks/form with an empty title re-renders the form with "Title is required" and persists nothing."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {"title": "", "projectId": 1})
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertIn("Title is required", r.body)
        self.assertEqual("4", sql("SELECT count(*) FROM task"))

    def test_s_form_011_create_with_whitespace_only_title_is_refused_without_mutation(self):
        """S-FORM-011 — A whitespace-only title is refused like an empty one and persists nothing."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {"title": "   ", "projectId": 1})
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertIn("Title is required", r.body)
        self.assertEqual("4", sql("SELECT count(*) FROM task"))

    def test_s_form_012_create_without_project_is_refused_without_mutation(self):
        """S-FORM-012 — POST /tasks/form without projectId is refused with "A task must belong to an existing project" and persists nothing."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {"title": "No project"})
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertIn("A task must belong to an existing project", r.body)
        self.assertEqual("4", sql("SELECT count(*) FROM task"))

    def test_s_form_013_create_with_unknown_project_is_refused_without_mutation(self):
        """S-FORM-013 — A projectId that does not exist is refused with the project error and persists nothing."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {"title": "Ghost project", "projectId": 999})
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertIn("A task must belong to an existing project", r.body)
        self.assertEqual("4", sql("SELECT count(*) FROM task"))

    def test_s_form_014_create_with_malformed_project_id_yields_500(self):
        """S-FORM-014 [characterization] — A non-numeric projectId (abc) surfaces a 500 before validation can run."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {"title": "Bad project", "projectId": "abc"})
        self.assertEqual(500, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("4", sql("SELECT count(*) FROM task"))

    def test_s_form_015_create_with_malformed_due_date_yields_500(self):
        """S-FORM-015 [characterization] — A non-ISO dueDate (not-a-date) surfaces a 500 (uncaught IllegalArgumentException)."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {"title": "Bad date", "projectId": 1, "dueDate": "not-a-date"})
        self.assertEqual(500, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("4", sql("SELECT count(*) FROM task"))

    def test_s_form_016_create_with_unknown_assignee_violates_fk_yields_500(self):
        """S-FORM-016 [characterization] — A nonexistent assigneeId surfaces a 500 (FK violation wrapped in a RuntimeException); no task is created."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {"title": "Ghost assignee", "projectId": 1, "assigneeId": 999})
        self.assertEqual(500, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("4", sql("SELECT count(*) FROM task"))

    def test_s_form_017_new_task_is_open_even_when_status_field_is_posted(self):
        """S-FORM-017 — New tasks always start OPEN; there is no way to create a task directly in another status."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {"title": "Sneaky status", "projectId": 1, "status": "DONE"})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("OPEN", tasks_table()[5]["status"])

    def test_s_form_025_title_at_column_width_boundary_persists_and_over_boundary_is_refused(self):
        """S-FORM-010 — A title of exactly 256 chars (the VARCHAR(256) column-width boundary) is accepted with 302, persists at full length and displays on the detail page; 257 chars exceeds the column and yields 500 without creating a row."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {"title": "A" * 256, "projectId": 1})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        saved_id = int(re.search(r"id=(\d+)", r.location).group(1))
        self.assertEqual("A" * 256, tasks_table()[saved_id]["title"])
        detail = self.client.get("/tasks/form", {"id": saved_id})
        self.assertEqual(200, detail.status)
        self.assertIn("A" * 256, detail.body)
        before = tasks_table()
        r_over = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {"title": "B" * 257, "projectId": 1})
        self.assertEqual(500, r_over.status)
        self.assertEqual(before, tasks_table(),
                         "the over-boundary title must not have created or changed any row")


class TaskEditTests(TrackerTestCase):

    def test_s_form_018_edit_persists_field_changes_and_redirects_to_detail(self):
        """S-FORM-018 — POST /tasks/form with an existing id updates title, description, priority, assignee and due date, then redirects to that task's detail with saved=1."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {
            "id": 1, "title": "Renamed", "description": "Updated",
            "priority": "LOW", "projectId": 1, "assigneeId": 2,
            "dueDate": "2026-09-30"})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertIn("/tasks/form?id=1", r.location)
        self.assertIn("saved=1", r.location)
        row = tasks_table()[1]
        self.assertEqual("Renamed", row["title"])
        self.assertEqual("LOW", row["priority"])
        self.assertEqual(2, row["assignee_id"])
        self.assertEqual("2026-09-30", row["due_date"])

    def test_s_form_019_edit_preserves_status(self):
        """S-FORM-019 — Editing a task never changes its status (an IN_PROGRESS task stays IN_PROGRESS)."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {
            "id": 2, "title": "Wire up SSO v2", "priority": "HIGH",
            "projectId": 1, "assigneeId": 3})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertEqual("IN_PROGRESS", tasks_table()[2]["status"])
        self.assertEqual("Wire up SSO v2", tasks_table()[2]["title"])

    def test_s_form_020_edit_does_not_move_project(self):
        """S-FORM-020 — Editing keeps the original project even when a different projectId is posted (update() never writes project_id)."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {
            "id": 1, "title": "Still on Apollo", "projectId": 2})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertEqual(1, tasks_table()[1]["project_id"])

    def test_s_form_021_edit_can_clear_assignee_and_due_date(self):
        """S-FORM-021 — Posting empty assignee and due date on edit persists NULLs (fields are nullable)."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {
            "id": 1, "title": "Unassigned", "projectId": 1,
            "assigneeId": "", "dueDate": ""})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        row = tasks_table()[1]
        self.assertIsNone(row["assignee_id"])
        self.assertIsNone(row["due_date"])

    def test_s_form_022_edit_with_blank_title_is_refused_without_mutation(self):
        """S-FORM-022 — Editing with an empty title is refused with "Title is required" and leaves the task untouched."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {"id": 1, "title": "", "projectId": 1})
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertIn("Title is required", r.body)
        self.assertEqual("Design login page", tasks_table()[1]["title"])

    def test_s_form_023_edit_with_unknown_project_is_refused_without_mutation(self):
        """S-FORM-023 — Editing with a nonexistent projectId is refused and leaves the task untouched."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {"id": 1, "title": "X", "projectId": 999})
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertIn("A task must belong to an existing project", r.body)
        self.assertEqual("Design login page", tasks_table()[1]["title"])

    def test_s_form_024_edit_nonexistent_id_creates_nothing(self):
        """S-FORM-024 [characterization] — POSTing a form with id=999 and valid fields runs an UPDATE that matches no rows: no error, no new task, no mutation."""
        self.client.login(MEMBER)
        r = self.client.post("/tasks/form", {"id": 999, "title": "Ghost edit", "projectId": 1})
        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
        self.assertIn("id=999", r.location)
        self.assertEqual("4", sql("SELECT count(*) FROM task"))
        self.assertEqual("OPEN", tasks_table()[1]["status"],
                         "no task may have been retitled by the ghost update")
tests/test_task_list.py

Raw file

"""Batch 4a — /tasks status+assignee filters and every whitelisted/default sort token.

Seed order facts (from app/src/main/resources/db/seed.sql, tasks 1..4):
  default / sort=updated -> updated_at DESC -> [4, 3, 2, 1]
  sort=status            -> status ASC (alpha), updated DESC tiebreak -> [4, 2, 1, 3]
  sort=priority          -> HIGH < MEDIUM < LOW, updated DESC tiebreak -> [2, 1, 3, 4]
  sort=due / due_date    -> due_date ASC with NULLS LAST, updated DESC tiebreak -> [3, 1, 2, 4]
  sort=title             -> title ASC, id ASC tiebreak -> [4, 1, 3, 2]
  sort=id                -> id ASC -> [1, 2, 3, 4]
"""
import re

from tests.helpers import TrackerTestCase, sql, tasks_table

ROW_RE = re.compile(r'<a href="/tasks/form\?id=(\d+)">([^<]+)</a>')


def listed_ids(body):
    return [int(m) for m, _ in ROW_RE.findall(body)]


def listed_titles(body):
    return [t for _, t in ROW_RE.findall(body)]


class TaskListOrderTests(TrackerTestCase):
    """The sort whitelist in TaskDao.orderClause: updated, status, priority, due, due_date, title, id."""

    def test_s_list_001_default_list_is_most_recently_updated_first(self):
        """S-LIST-001 — GET /tasks with no sort parameter lists all tasks ordered updated_at DESC: [4, 3, 2, 1]."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks")
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertEqual([4, 3, 2, 1], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_002_sort_updated_equals_default(self):
        """S-LIST-002 — sort=updated is an explicit whitelisted token equal to the default order: [4, 3, 2, 1]."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"sort": "updated"})
        self.assertEqual([4, 3, 2, 1], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_003_sort_status_orders_alphabetically_with_updated_tiebreak(self):
        """S-LIST-003 — sort=status orders by status alphabetically (DONE, IN_PROGRESS, OPEN, PENDING_APPROVAL), updated DESC within: [4, 2, 1, 3]."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"sort": "status"})
        self.assertEqual([4, 2, 1, 3], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_004_sort_priority_is_semantic_high_medium_low(self):
        """S-LIST-004 — sort=priority orders HIGH before MEDIUM before LOW (semantic, not alphabetical), updated DESC within: [2, 1, 3, 4]."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"sort": "priority"})
        self.assertEqual([2, 1, 3, 4], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_005_sort_due_puts_null_due_dates_last(self):
        """S-LIST-005 — sort=due orders due_date ascending with NULL last, updated DESC within: [3, 1, 2, 4]."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"sort": "due"})
        self.assertEqual([3, 1, 2, 4], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_006_sort_due_date_token_is_whitelisted_like_due(self):
        """S-LIST-006 — sort=due_date is an accepted alias of sort=due and produces the same order: [3, 1, 2, 4]."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"sort": "due_date"})
        self.assertEqual([3, 1, 2, 4], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_007_sort_title_orders_alphabetically_with_id_tiebreak(self):
        """S-LIST-007 — sort=title orders by title alphabetically then id: [4, 1, 3, 2]."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"sort": "title"})
        self.assertEqual([4, 1, 3, 2], listed_ids(r.body))
        self.assertEqual(["Archive old data", "Design login page",
                          "Migrate invoices", "Wire up SSO"], listed_titles(r.body))

    def test_s_list_008_sort_id_orders_by_identifier(self):
        """S-LIST-008 — sort=id orders by identifier ascending: [1, 2, 3, 4]."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"sort": "id"})
        self.assertEqual([1, 2, 3, 4], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_009_unknown_sort_token_falls_back_to_updated_desc(self):
        """S-LIST-009 [characterization] — A non-whitelisted sort token (sort=; DROP TABLE task) silently falls back to the default updated DESC order instead of erroring."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"sort": "; DROP TABLE task"})
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertEqual([4, 3, 2, 1], listed_ids(r.body))
        self.assertEqual("4", sql("SELECT count(*) FROM task"))

    def test_s_list_010_mutation_bumps_task_to_top_of_default_order(self):
        """S-LIST-010 — After a transition refreshes a task's updated_at, that task leads the default (updated DESC) list."""
        self.client.login()
        self.client.post("/tasks/action", {"id": 1, "action": "start"})
        r = self.client.get("/tasks")
        self.assertEqual([1, 4, 3, 2], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_011_list_shows_all_columns_for_each_row(self):
        """S-LIST-011 — Each list row shows id, title, project name, assignee name, status, priority and due date."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks")
        for token in ("Design login page", "Apollo", "Devon Member", "OPEN", "HIGH", "2026-06-15"):
            self.assertIn(token, r.body)


class TaskFilterTests(TrackerTestCase):
    """status and assignee narrow the list; values are parameterized."""

    def test_s_list_025_empty_sort_parameter_uses_the_default_order(self):
        """S-LIST-025 [characterization] — sort= (empty string) is treated as the default updated DESC order and the page labels the active sort "updated"."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"sort": ""})
        self.assertEqual([4, 3, 2, 1], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_026_empty_assignee_parameter_means_no_filter(self):
        """S-LIST-026 — assignee= (empty string) applies no assignee filter: all four tasks in default order."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"assignee": ""})
        self.assertEqual([4, 3, 2, 1], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_012_status_filter_open_returns_only_open_tasks(self):
        """S-LIST-012 — GET /tasks?status=OPEN returns only the OPEN task [1]."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"status": "OPEN"})
        self.assertEqual([1], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_013_status_filter_in_progress(self):
        """S-LIST-013 — GET /tasks?status=IN_PROGRESS returns only [2]."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"status": "IN_PROGRESS"})
        self.assertEqual([2], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_014_status_filter_pending_approval(self):
        """S-LIST-014 — GET /tasks?status=PENDING_APPROVAL returns only [3]."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"status": "PENDING_APPROVAL"})
        self.assertEqual([3], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_015_status_filter_done(self):
        """S-LIST-015 — GET /tasks?status=DONE returns only [4]."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"status": "DONE"})
        self.assertEqual([4], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_016_unknown_status_value_returns_empty_list(self):
        """S-LIST-016 — A status value outside the enum (e.g. CANCELLED) yields an empty list, not an error."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"status": "CANCELLED"})
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertEqual([], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_017_empty_status_filter_returns_all_tasks(self):
        """S-LIST-017 — An empty status parameter means no filter: all four tasks, default order."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"status": ""})
        self.assertEqual([4, 3, 2, 1], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_018_assignee_filter_returns_that_users_tasks_only(self):
        """S-LIST-018 — GET /tasks?assignee=3 returns tasks 1, 2, 3 in updated DESC order [3, 2, 1]."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"assignee": 3})
        self.assertEqual([3, 2, 1], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_019_assignee_filter_for_admin(self):
        """S-LIST-019 — GET /tasks?assignee=1 returns only task 4 (Ada's single assignment)."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"assignee": 1})
        self.assertEqual([4], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_020_assignee_filter_unknown_id_returns_empty(self):
        """S-LIST-020 — GET /tasks?assignee=999 returns an empty list (no such person)."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"assignee": 999})
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertEqual([], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_021_combined_status_and_assignee_filter(self):
        """S-LIST-021 — status and assignee combine conjunctively: status=IN_PROGRESS&assignee=3 returns [2]."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"status": "IN_PROGRESS", "assignee": 3})
        self.assertEqual([2], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_022_combined_filter_matching_nothing_returns_empty(self):
        """S-LIST-022 — status=DONE&assignee=3 matches no task and yields an empty list without error."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"status": "DONE", "assignee": 3})
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        self.assertEqual([], listed_ids(r.body))

    def test_s_list_023_malformed_assignee_filter_yields_500(self):
        """S-LIST-023 [characterization] — assignee=abc surfaces a 500 (uncaught NumberFormatException in TaskListServlet)."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"assignee": "abc"})
        self.assertEqual(500, r.status)

    def test_s_list_024_task_titles_are_rendered_unescaped_in_html(self):
        """S-LIST-024 [characterization] — A task title containing HTML (<b>…) is rendered raw by the list JSP (EL output is not escaped). Defect candidate: stored XSS via task title."""
        self.client.login()
        self.client.post("/tasks/form", {"title": "<b>bold</b>", "projectId": 1})
        r = self.client.get("/tasks")
        self.assertIn("<b>bold</b>", r.body,
                      "observed: raw HTML reaches the browser; intended requirement would be escaping")

    def test_s_list_027_toolbar_renders_six_sort_options_and_reflects_selection(self):
        """S-QUERY-008 — GET /tasks?sort=priority&status=OPEN renders the toolbar with exactly the six whitelisted sort options (updated, status, priority, due, title, id), priority selected, and the OPEN status filter selected (render-only)."""
        self.client.login()
        r = self.client.get("/tasks", {"sort": "priority", "status": "OPEN"})
        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
        sort_select = re.search(r'<select id="sort".*?</select>', r.body, re.S).group(0)
        self.assertEqual(["updated", "status", "priority", "due", "title", "id"],
                         re.findall(r'<option value="([^"]+)"', sort_select))
        self.assertIn('<option value="priority" selected', sort_select)
        status_select = re.search(r'<select id="status".*?</select>', r.body, re.S).group(0)
        self.assertIn('<option value="OPEN" selected', status_select)

    def test_s_data_001_masthead_status_badge_and_null_placeholders_render(self):
        """S-DATA-008 — Authenticated pages render the shared masthead with user identity and logout (GET /tasks as dev), and the task 4 detail page shows the status-DONE badge, the (unassigned) option, and the — placeholder for its null due date (render-only)."""
        self.client.login()
        listing = self.client.get("/tasks")
        self.assertEqual(200, listing.status)
        self.assertIn('class="brand"', listing.body)
        self.assertIn("Devon Member (MEMBER)", listing.body)
        self.assertIn('href="/logout"', listing.body)
        detail = self.client.get("/tasks/form", {"id": 4})
        self.assertEqual(200, detail.status)
        self.assertIn("statusbadge status-DONE", detail.body)
        self.assertIn("(unassigned)", detail.body)
        self.assertIn("\u2014", detail.body,
                      "em-dash placeholder must render in task 4's null due-date cell")
